# VAMILY -- THE MEETING HALL (Paolo 9/4/26, LOCKED)
# "the chats should have a place like a meeting hall in the files, a job board,
# a center of a room where they start off and pick up their jobs... why should I
# be passing them any information other than saying VAMILY."
# THIS FILE IS THAT ROOM. THE RULES LIVE HERE, NOT IN ANY CHAT'S MEMORY.

## READ THIS FRONT PAGE EVERY SINGLE TIME YOU HEAR THE WORD VAMILY
Not once. Every time. Rules change on this page and nowhere else, so a chat that
remembers last week's rules is wrong. Paolo never pastes anything; he types the
one word, and this page is the briefing.

0. VAMILY is a keyword. It has nothing to do with families or dynasties. It means:
   read this page, find your section below, do your job from it.
1. Paolo talks ONLY to the coordinator (00 MASTER COORDINATOR, the manager). Every
   other chat gets the one word and nothing else. Never ask him anything. A ruling
   you need goes in your handoff block in 00_START_HERE_NEXT_SESSION.md as
   [PENDING Paolo]; the coordinator carries it to him.
2. FIND YOUR SECTION by this chat's number and name (the list is right below). A
   fresh chat with no name takes the first line marked UNCLAIMED, writes CLAIMED on
   it, commits, and that is its role for life. AS OF 9/14: the one UNCLAIMED line is
   20 PORTRAIT (Paolo 9/14: "we might need to open up a new chat for the portrait");
   PLUMBER is claimed. A brand-new chat is PORTRAIT.
3. READ YOUR SECTION'S MODE AND STATE LINES FIRST. STATE says what is built, what
   is not, and what nobody has checked, so you know what you do not know.
4. Every job carries a TWO-WORD label in [brackets], for him. The name after it is
   the job. A BB- name's full text is that row in BOHEMIA_BACKLOG.md; any other
   job's one-line brief here IS the job.
5. IF YOU ALREADY HOLD A CLAIMED JOB, VAMILY MEANS CONTINUE IT, from where your
   handoff block says you stopped. A job takes as many rounds as it takes; nobody
   expects one round. Otherwise take the FIRST line marked OPEN in your section,
   change it to CLAIMED <date> <session>, commit and push BEFORE you start.
6. MODE: BUILD means build it, prove it on the real surface (the walked city and
   the demo, on a phone if you can), re-cut the demo, run your lane's gates, then
   change the line to SHIPPED <date> <commit> and push. Mark SHIPPED only when the
   job's own ship test is met; a half-done job marked SHIPPED is worse than an open
   one, never rush to close a line.
   MODE: RESEARCH means DO NOT IMPLEMENT: one research round on the question (the records call it a "day"; it is one VAMILY, not a calendar day, and you never call it a day to Paolo), both
   angles (the best games ever made, and the real world), one finding that proves
   us wrong, measured against our repo, a written record, test material tagged
   draft:true in a bank file never in the game, a ROUTED section; mark SHIPPED
   with the record path.
   MODE: PARKED means nobody touches it until Paolo reopens it.
7. Every mechanic is 120 BPM friendly or it is not done. A row is not shipped
   until it is in the walked surface AND the demo.
8. STANDING DUTIES, every round: VAMILY first; play it before you call it shipped;
   his bugs beat your queue; run your gates, never ship red; rewrite your handoff
   block before you end. ADDED 9/13: if your diff touches engine/, rebuild the two
   derived slices (the RUN_CURRENT slice and the demo) in the same commit; PLUMBER
   found the RUN slice drifting +45/-6 behind two engine ships that never rebuilt it,
   and the freshness gate was red for everybody because of it. ADDED 9/13 (PLUMBER 5f9a7498 measured 93 commits that deleted another lane's newest handoff block, 80 blocks lost, 42 still missing, and it was never the rebases: a lane reads the file at the start of its round and writes the whole file back an hour later): RE-READ THE HANDOFF IMMEDIATELY BEFORE YOU WRITE IT, never from the copy you read at the start of your round. The handoff gate now refuses a commit that deletes a block HEAD carries; tools/bohemia_handoff_recover.js puts eaten blocks back.
9. END EVERY ROUND by replying to him with the two-word label and one short line:
   "continuing, about N of M" or "shipped" or "queue empty".
10. Only the coordinator adds jobs here (one exception: EYES AND EARS may add a
    single [eyes: two words] bounce-back line on a defect it found in a SHIPPED
    item). Lanes change status words and nothing else. The SHIPPED lines are the
    history.
Full law: laws/BOHEMIA_ADDENDUM_THE_VAMILY_WORD_9_4_26.md and
laws/BOHEMIA_ADDENDUM_THE_CENTRAL_CHAT_9_4_26.md

11. A SHIPPED LINE KEEPS THE RULING IT ANSWERED (coordinator 9/12, from EYES E16). Measured: an OPEN line cites one of Paolo's rulings 34% of the time, a SHIPPED line only 12%, because the line gets rewritten as a result summary when it lands and his words go with it. From now, when you mark a line SHIPPED you keep its [two-word label] AND the "Paolo <date>" citation exactly as the OPEN line carried them; you may ADD the sha and one result clause, you never delete the ruling. The link between what he said and what got built is the point of this board.
12. A DEPENDENCY ON A LINE IS A PREMISE, NOT A GATE (coordinator 9/13, from FACTIONS [tracks read]: "the named blocker was not the blocker, sixth row running"). When a line says "after X" or "with X", the lane MEASURES FIRST whether X is actually needed and builds if it is not. Six rows in a row shipped without the blocker the coordinator named. Nothing on this board waits on a line the coordinator wrote; only on a measurement the lane made.
13. "I RAN THE SUITE" MEANS TWO THINGS AND ONLY TWO (coordinator 9/13, from three lanes measuring the same wall: 331 of 603 gates NEVER RAN in a 45-minute budget, 56 of 372 that did run were red, the floor is 71 minutes; records/BOHEMIA_COORDINATOR_SWING_HOW_BIG_SHOPS_RUN_A_SUITE_THAT_CANNOT_FINISH_9_13_26.md). (a) You ran the PRE-PUSH PASS, the gates that read the files in your diff, and it was green. (b) You read THE SUITE LINE, the last full run PLUMBER posts on this front page (sha, ran, green, red, never ran, flaked), and you name which of its reds are yours. Nobody runs the whole 70 minutes alone any more, and nobody writes "suite green" over gates that never ran. A gate on the QUARANTINE list still runs and never decides a ship. Until PLUMBER posts the first line, the honest sentence is "pre-push pass green; full suite unmeasured since <sha>".
THE SUITE LINE: 9/14, ad23d875 (posted by the coordinator off PLUMBER's run; PLUMBER [suite line] makes the machine post it): box 0.89x of baseline, 632 gates ran, 525 GREEN, 107 RED, 0 never ran, 12,925 s of gate work, floor 71.3 min. FACTION ARC 557.7 s against a 600 s cap, not safe. QUARANTINE: none listed yet. Rule 13's honest sentence for every lane until the next line: 'pre-push pass green; full suite: 107 red at ad23d875, mine are ...'.

ONE LINE FOR RUN AND ONE FOR LIFE+CITY, FROM PLUMBER (named, not fixed: this lane may not touch slices/ content or any feature).
 RUN: the demo's first 13 seconds of PLAY, measured on a 4x-throttled phone profile, run at about 10 frames a second and then snap to 57. That is after the door, not before it, so a loading screen does not cover it. The door itself opens at 52.9 s at 4x against his 5 s goal. Reproduce with: node tools/bohemia_five_minutes.js --throttle 4
 LIFE+CITY: the same 13 seconds are the city frame's first paint. The per-second curve is in records/BOHEMIA_NOT_RUNNING_SMOOTHLY_THE_FIRST_THIRTEEN_SECONDS_9_15_26.md and the driver now stamps the door so you can line your own work up against it (d.firstPaintMs()).

*** ROUND 2, 9/15 -- PAOLO'S OWN SENTENCE, ANSWERED WITH A NUMBER: "IT'S KINDA NOT RUNNING AS SMOOTHLY AS I WOULD LIKE, MAYBE IT'S CAUSE THINGS ARE LOADING IN REAL TIME." (PLUMBER, ruling 5, row [sixty fps]. Record: records/BOHEMIA_NOT_RUNNING_SMOOTHLY_THE_FIRST_THIRTEEN_SECONDS_9_15_26.md) ***
 HE MEETS THE WORST PART OF THE GAME FIRST, AND THE BOX WAS HIDING IT. This container is not a handset; every fps number this fleet ever posted was taken on a machine several times faster than his phone. The walk now takes a CPU throttle, and a ladder was run in one window:
   1x   door 13.4 s   58.4 fps over the walk   53.3 fps in the first 15 s   worst stall 1.2 s
   4x   door 52.9 s   53.4 fps over the walk    9.8 fps in the first 15 s   worst stall 8.8 s
   6x   door 79.4 s   34.9 fps over the walk   11.0 fps in the first 15 s   worst stall 8.1 s
 At 4x, second by second: 4, 8, 6, 4, 6, 8, 7, 10, 7, 7, 9, 7, 5, 2 -- THEN 57, 56, 57. THIRTEEN SECONDS AT ABOUT TEN FRAMES A SECOND, THEN IT SNAPS TO FIFTY-SEVEN. The 6x run has the same shape. At 1x the dip is three seconds and small, which is why nobody has ever seen it. A whole-walk average buries it completely, and thirteen seconds is a sixth of his five minutes and the first thing he touches. THIS IS NOT MINE TO FIX: it lives in the demo shell's boot and the city frame's first paint. ONE LINE FOR RUN and ONE FOR LIFE+CITY.
 AND HIS THEORY, NAMED: the door opens after 3 files; then 30 more files and 75.9 MB stream in behind him while he plays, the last landing 227 s after the door (at 4x: 31 files, 80.9 MB, last at 311 s, past the end of his five minutes). That is the demo shell's warm-fetch queue working as designed; the SIZE is what nobody was watching. Twelve chunks are asked for twice in the first 75 s. NEW GATE, in the suite as LOADING IN PLAY: gates/loading_during_play_gate.js, a ratchet pinned off two of its own runs (63.9 MB, 27 files in a 75 s window), four floors before any ceiling, GREEN 6/0.
 WHAT I COULD NOT CHECK, SAID OUT LOUD: the test server sets no cache headers and this container's network policy blocks the live site, so whether a real browser re-downloads those chunks or reads them from disk is UNKNOWN from here. Either way the bytes are parsed on the main thread while he plays.
 AND A TEST I BUILT AND THREW AWAY BEFORE IT REACHED THIS PAGE: I tried to prove his theory by splitting the walk into loading seconds and quiet seconds. Two ways of lining up the load clock with the frame clock gave two answers off the SAME run (45.3 vs 55.3, gap 10.0; and 51.7 vs 55.4, gap 3.7). A test that changes its verdict with its arithmetic is not evidence, so it is deleted. The second-by-second curve above needs no alignment at all.

THE FOUR NUMBERS UNDER THE BREAK LIST (PLUMBER posts them every round, row [demo errors]; rule 14 asked for them by name. Walk: 300 s on a 390x844 phone, box 1.04x of baseline, driven by tools/bohemia_drive_the_demo.js per 14(g). Record: records/BOHEMIA_FIVE_MINUTES_THE_CONTROL_WALK_9_14_26.md, raw records/BOHEMIA_FIVE_MINUTES.json)
 PAGE ERRORS         0   nothing threw in five minutes, three walks running. Routed to nobody.
 STALLS              1   one gap over a beat in 300 s, worst 1,550 ms. PLUMBER'S OWN, not routed: this lane owns "60 on a real phone".
 FRAMES UNDER 60  57.8   fps, and 194 frames of 17,402 arrived so late a whole frame went missing (1.1%). PLUMBER'S OWN, not routed.
 DEAD TAPS   NOT MEASURED YET, AND I AM SAYING SO INSTEAD OF POSTING A FOURTH GUESS. Four versions this round, three of them wrong (0 of 45 landed; then 162 of 230; then 48 of 77 twice, and the fix I shipped between those two changed nothing, so that diagnosis was wrong too). STOP PRODUCING, 7/26: a fourth version means you already failed, so stop and say so.
   WHAT THE FOURTH VERSION DID PROVE, and it is the finding of the round: A SCREEN DIFF IS NOT A TEST OF WHETHER A CONTROL WORKS, IN EITHER DIRECTION. The tool now runs a CONTROL WALK (--no-taps): the whole five minutes, same order, same waits, same pictures, PRESSING NOTHING. Pressing everything = 48 dead of 77. Pressing nothing = 0 dead of 69. So the counter does not invent numbers. But the same run proves the demo REPAINTS CONSTANTLY, so two identical pictures do not mean "the button did nothing", they mean the surface STOPPED MOVING for 900 ms -- and it is not a freeze either, because the frame watcher saw ONE stall in either walk. Then all six names were pressed one at a time from a clean door and photographed at 120/300/900/2000 ms: MUSIC (#musbtn), SAVE (#savebtn), PHONE1 (#phonebtn) and SUBURB · ON FOOT (#hslot) ALL MOVE THE SCREEN; OUTFIT and DAY 1 · 13:57 are not even on screen at the door, and two of the six are status readouts the driver's controls() hands over as buttons because it sweeps every div with short text.
   THIS IS THE OTHER HALF OF RULE 14(h). QUESTS proved a diff reads FALSE LIFE (a card closes on a tap it does not know). This proves it reads FALSE DEATH. What settles it is QUESTS' shape, not a patch on mine: require THE PANEL TO STILL BE OPEN AND ITS WORDS TO HAVE MOVED. Words do not repaint on their own. That is the next cut of this instrument.
   ONE LINE FOR EYES E26, not a fix by this lane: your item 2 names two demo buttons dead on "no change in words or pixels for 1.2 s". That is the instrument that just failed here. It does not mean your buttons work; it means the evidence is the kind that reads false death on this surface.

15. ONE VOTE TAB (PAOLO 9/14, LOCKED; laws/BOHEMIA_ADDENDUM_ONE_VOTE_TAB_AND_THE_PORTRAIT_CHAT_9_14_26.md). "We need one central tab in the fucking demo where everything from sounds to portrait to hair to everything that is new, where I vote on it... after I vote on something it needs to stop presenting itself." (a) Everything that wants his thumb goes to the VOTE tab in the DEMO, behind the gear, and nowhere else: songs, faces, haircuts, outfits, tiles, animations, REDONE animations, lines. No lane builds its own judge page again; the old eight present nothing new. (b) A VOTE CONSUMES THE ITEM: it leaves the tab the moment he votes and never renders again under that id; the verdict is written to records/ as .txt AND to the registry the tab reads. A redo is a new item that names the old one and quotes why he killed it. (c) Approved goes into the game and its own tab the same round by the lane that owns it, without asking him twice; killed goes to the graveyard, except clips, which are redone and come back. (d) UI owns the shell, the queue, the registry and the vanish gate; every lane registers its candidates and reads verdicts back; RUN cuts it into the demo. Tab: VOTE, in the demo, behind the gear.

ONE STEP IS ONE LOT, on the street and on the fight board, for the player, every character and every enemy; bodies are drawn LARGER on both (default: about half a lot tall at walk zoom, he corrects it in play); 120 BPM and one step per beat unchanged; the clock per step is re-derived from walking speed so distances, days and rent stay true and real-time travel gets faster by the multiple. ONE NUMBER IN ONE PLACE that the street, the fight, the bodies, the zoom seam and reach all read. NOT A REBUILD: the art under the feet stays, the movement lattice coarsens to lots. THE STRANGER'S LIST (EYES E26 posts it every round; walked cold on a phone profile, observations only, never a score. Round 6, 9/15, full record records/BOHEMIA_EYES_E26_ROUND_6_HE_WAS_LOOKING_AT_A_FROZEN_FRAME_9_15_26.md. Walked on BUILD 9/14p, the cut he played)
 0. *** HE DID SEE NOBODY, AND THE POPULATION IS NOT THE PROBLEM. HE WAS LOOKING AT A FROZEN FRAME. *** Timestamped BY THE PAGE ITSELF, six controls green: 0.5 s the shell draws, 5.6 s the city draws, 9.4 s THE GAME FREEZES FOR 6.0 SECONDS, 13.8 s the first human body is painted just off the screen edge, 17.2 s THE FIRST BODY LANDS INSIDE THE SCREEN, 20.5 s THE GAME FREEZES FOR 11.1 SECONDS. His bar in the routing note is ten seconds; it is 17.2. In a second run the freeze was 13.7 s and started at 17.2 s, the same second the first body landed. So the first appearance of people in this game happens inside, or on the lip of, a freeze long enough to read as broken.
 1. TOTAL FROZEN: 28.9 SECONDS OUT OF THE FIRST 300, ACROSS 42 SEPARATE FREEZES. That is the measured form of his other sentence, 'not running as smoothly as I would like, maybe it's cause things are loading in real time'. It is not a feeling, the thread stops. -> PLUMBER [sixty fps].
 2. AND LIFE+CITY'S GATE IS NOT LYING, WHICH IS WORTH SAYING PLAINLY. After the build, bodies are painted on 135 of 135 samples, up to 20 at once, and all 1,130 body draws land INSIDE the screen rectangle rather than beyond the cull; the engine's own near-list agrees at up to 11. THE PREMISE IN THE ROUTING NOTE RESOLVES TO ITS SECOND BRANCH: the crowd loads after he has already looked, and the game is frozen when it arrives. NOTHING NEEDS ADDING TO THE POPULATION. -> PEOPLE [a human being]: the work is EARLIER and UNFROZEN, not MORE.
 3. 55 FIGHT WORDS EXIST IN THE DEMO AND A PLAYER CAN READ ZERO OF THEM. In five minutes nothing visible ever mentions a fight, an enemy, an attack or a hostile. The words were written: 'THE FIGHT IS OVER', 'YOU SWING AND MISS', 'STREAK 0 - ENEMIES 3'. All 55 are inside hidden panels, the sound factory and the fight's own dev controls, zero-sized or display none. His sentence is exactly right and it is NOT a missing-words problem: they are behind a door he never opens. -> COMBAT [start a fight].
 4. THE STANDING WALK, THIRD INDEPENDENT CONFIRMATION on this cut: the same three dead rows on the day card ('Half of it now, before I go', 'I will go first, on something small', 'I'LL TAKE IT'), GET UP still reads THE PANEL CLOSED so it proves nothing under 14(h), no fight surface in five minutes, first tappable 3.4 s, 0 page errors, 0 failed requests. Still with LIFE + CITY as [eyes: shape rows].
 5. AND I MADE THE ROUND-4 MISTAKE AGAIN, TWO ROUNDS RUNNING, AND A CONTROL IS THE ONLY REASON IT DID NOT REACH THIS PAGE. My first cut polled for the people pass after the front-screen click; that click BLOCKS for about twelve seconds while the city builds, so it wrapped at 21.3 s and announced 'first body painted at 23.7 s, his ten-second bar not met'. 23.7 s was MY OWN START-UP. A second cut armed a trap before the page ran and the trap did not take (the pass ran ZERO times) because a global function declaration redefines the property instead of calling a setter. THE REAL LIMIT, now stated instead of estimated: there is ONE main thread, so while the build holds it no outside observer can read anything AND the game cannot paint either. The tool arms a heartbeat and a draw timestamp INSIDE the page before any page script runs, and the page reports the moment itself.
 6. AND MY WORD SWEEP WAS READING HIDDEN TEXT. It first reported 'something says FIGHT at 21.9 s', which would have answered his complaint with words he cannot see, the same error as calling a hidden control a working button. It keeps only visible, sized, in-viewport text now and prints both numbers, which is how item 3 above became a finding instead of a false pass.
 BLIND SPOTS, STATED: this does not judge pictures, so his three sentences about the car and the far zoom are not mine and I did not try. I cannot see the first 22 seconds from outside and I say so rather than estimating. AND ONE NEW LIMIT: 'a body painted inside the screen rectangle' is not 'a body a person notices' -- at the far zoom a body is a few pixels and this counts it the same as one filling a quarter of the screen. Next to measure, by the body's drawn size against the screen.

## WORLD  (02. the economy, the map, the towns)
MODE: BUILD  RULE 14 (9/13): ship to the alpha; never re-cut the demo; RUN cuts it. RULED 9/13 (records/BOHEMIA_RULING_NINE_DEFAULTS_THE_ECONOMY_WAS_CARRYING_9_13_26.md): nine defaults this lane's research was carrying as pending (first building = the pump; money supply measured off the map; a broken promise costs exclusion; seven-day market week; barter at a camp to a stranger; a ruin houses two households). Rows for them are written when the freeze lifts.
STATE: the purse ledger and payout pipe are built and called; PAYOUT and PRICES carry his 8/15 ONE denominated in his 9/4 BATTERY, every value tagged and untuned, so a day's work pays one battery and a bag of rice costs one on the walked surface and in the demo (9/5); PRODUCTION now has a caller -- LIFE+CITY's production tick pays a placed building's yield into the purse; ELECTRICITY now moves both ways; the map is 35 districts with 18 factions placed. NOTHING IS FREE ANY MORE (9/5): four frozen verbs drain three currencies -- the day eats food, the fight eats tape, the night eats power, asking eats clout -- all four posting on the walked surface and in the demo, each named on the reckoning card, a fifth verb refused and the amount out of the signature. HOLDING GROUND NOW COSTS (9/5): a plot HE built fronts a street feeder and that feeder is his, one battery a night per circuit, deduped by feeder so four shops on one street are one bill; a circuit he cannot pay for GOES DARK, stays dark, rides its own save key, and stops billing him (no debt spiral). The darkening lives inside the grid, whose status table is private, so at() is the only door and all ten readers of .live get it for free. Nothing calls relight(): what it costs to get your lights back is a price and prices are his. UNPROVEN, written down rather than glossed: I could not photograph the street going dark -- dousing all 173 lit circuits moved the aerial frame by zero pixels, which proves my probe was the broken instrument, not the grid. GAP CLOSED 9/12 by [rice clock] (0f793c2): buy() used to take the battery and land nothing, so the shop was a dead end and day:ate was REFUSED on day one; buying now goes through the purse's atomic convert(), the good really arrives in resources, and the hard sink moved one step later to the verb that eats it. Work -> battery -> rice -> eaten, closed, and the nightfall card counts the days nobody ate. EVERY PART OF VEGAS HAS AN OWNER (9/5): all 14 selectable factions have a SEAT that is a real market on the walked surface and in the demo, taking the valley from 2 markets to 16 and the first one from 38 cells away to 7. The TIER is derived from his own act1_power (thirds, top rounds up, 5/4/5) and lands Colorful -- the faction he named as the small one -- in CAMP with nothing tuned; act3_power flips four factions between acts, so the CENTURY RULE costs no new field. A camp is THINNER, never dearer: everything is one battery, so depth is the only axis his words give. SEATS and TIER both ship EMPTY. FIXED ON THE WAY: the 14 factions were seated in TWO places that disagreed by ninety cells (the loop's isAutoDistrict vs the walked surface's cityedit cat), so two answers to where the Mob lives; one rule in engine/bohemia_towns.js now and both surfaces are measured identical. Seats never land on ground that can be empty (8 measured kinds excluded), 0 unreachable across 84 seats on 6 seeds. EVERY BLOCK HAS A NAME ON IT (9/5): the grid's circuit owner was a CATEGORY, so one circuit in five read as the generic word "faction" and the seam test made a Mob block and a Cartel block the same block. The name now comes off the town seats -- a faction holds the ground around its own town and a FORTRESS REACHES FURTHER THAN A CAMP, off the REACH table that already sized a town. MEASURED and it shaped the design: across five seeds every lit border is a CATEGORY border and two NAMED factions are never adjacent, because neighbouring circuits share a catchment -- so the lit circuits are the TELL and the territory is the catchment, which covers all 9,216 cells with 0 left over, 14 factions, fortresses holding the most and camps slivers. The player opens on Mob ground. ROADS ARE FAST (9/5): measured first, 29 paved cells and 29 cells of broken ground both cost 2 minutes, so every street in the valley was mechanically scenery. Pavement (asphalt + concrete, off the footstep classifier so the ground that SOUNDS like asphalt walks like it) now costs half; broken ground is untouched, so the 8.6 km day the row calls a good number is unchanged and a day on the roads covers 17.1 km across a 9.2 km valley -- you can cross it in a day if you stay on roads and cannot if you do not. The encounter director is handed the seconds REALLY spent, or his 90-second gap would have halved on pavement. The card says how far he got, not just how many steps. THE RUNG IS A DOOR (9/6): bohemia_mandate.js has carried canBuild() since 8/11 with ZERO callers in the game, so crossing a rung paid nothing. The two-month PENDING only blocked because everybody read "grant" as a NUMBER -- his own list offers "cost multipliers? unlock tiers? restriction removal?" and the third is a DOOR already written in the module header in his words. Rung two now opens building on ground a faction holds; MAYOR stays [PENDING Paolo] and does not block it. TWO THINGS MEASURING CAUGHT: the player boots with NOT ONE friendly faction, so a naive permit walls him in on day one (answered by LIGHT=TERRITORY and NOBODY PATROLS THE DARK -- a faction can only stop you where somebody is standing); and the first cut asked whether the PLOT was lit and refused ZERO real builds, because circuits only run along STREET cells. 63 of 580 buildable plots gated at rung one, 89% open, all of them open at MANDATE. THE WORLD GETS ORGANISED (9/6): factions hostile to EACH OTHER in his graph who are BOTH hostile to the player stop fighting each other -- a graph edit with no numbers in the module at all, no threshold (that would be an N nobody ruled), no damage, derived so making peace with one dissolves it and his feud survives underneath. The STANDING card says "AGAINST YOU: Cartel + Remnants" and that nobody got stronger. COALITIONS ships EMPTY. MEASURED: his PAIRS table is 5 pairs of which only 2 are hostile, so earned feuds count too or the mechanic would be capped at his starting graph. ALSO FIXED: the STANDING card still said "Nobody holds this ground yet" -- false since BB-TURF -- and now names the holder. The card says "you crossed Mob into Homeless", and a block going dark names whose it was. HOLDS ships EMPTY. Colour stays his (COLOUR IS TERRITORY): there is no faction colour table the walked surface can reach, so the tell is the light and the name arrives in words.

(coordinator 9/13)  *** WORLD 9/15 MEASURED IT AND PUT IT BACK: THE PICKET CANNOT BE COUNTED, AND THE FIX IS PEOPLE'S LANE, NOT THIS ONE. *** Every part the row names is real except the one it turns on. MEASURED ON THE WALKED SURFACE: (a) THERE IS EXACTLY ONE RENT PAYER IN THE VALLEY AND IT IS THE PLAYER -- rentOn() is handed TURF_USED, which counts blocks the player walked, and nothing else calls it, so 'a whole block stops paying' cannot be twenty households withholding twenty rents; those rents do not exist. (b) BUT THE BLOCK IS REAL: 20 homes and 20 people on the block the player wakes on, with ids, households and homes. (c) AND THE GAME ALREADY HAS THE PICKET -- whoVouches() and whoWont() in bohemia_standing answer who stands with you, off real deeds with gossip and half-life applied. (d) AND SINCE [every pocket] 9/14 the owner CAN lose money, which is what this row was waiting for. SO I BUILT IT: the block holds the door when more of it vouches than won't (a majority is the shape of a picket, not a tuned number, so nothing ships a weight), the cut cannot be made to stick, and otherwise the light is bought back at his ruled ONE paid into the owner's purse. THEN THE ONE MEASUREMENT THAT KILLED IT: CT_MINDS IS EMPTY AND STAYS EMPTY. At the door 0 minds; after twelve hours of advanced game time, still 0. Minds are born when something touches ctMind(), which is the outfit cards, NOT people walking past. ZERO of the block's 20 residents has a mind, so whoVouches over the block returns nothing, always, and the picket cannot be counted. NOT A NUMBER NOBODY RULED -- the weights are fine (bohemia_clout fills 83 rows from the quest corpus at load, 0.6 to 1.2) -- it is that the PEOPLE have no minds. THE FIX IS GIVING A BLOCK'S RESIDENTS MINDS, WHICH IS 09 PEOPLE'S LANE, and building it here would cross one-system-one-session. Noted too: none of the five city deed kinds (claim:met, claim:refused, commit, favour, loan:short) is in the weight table, so a city deed is witnessed, remembered, retold and weighs nothing. records/BOHEMIA_BLOCK_STRIKES_THE_PICKET_CANNOT_BE_COUNTED_9_15_26.md

You bought food, the food did not exist, and you starved. buy() read SHOPPING as the hard sink; the four-verbs law says the verb that spends resources is day:ate, so SHOPPING MOVES VALUE AND EATING DESTROYS IT. It goes through the purse's own convert() now, atomic, which had sat there since 7/31 with zero callers. Work -> battery -> rice -> eaten, closed. AND HUNGER COUNTS AND SAYS, NEVER BITES: NO DAMAGE BEFORE THE DIAL is locked and the day loop wrote the same sentence, so the streak is read back out of the ledger, rises in words, names the thing to buy, and takes nothing off him. - SHIPPED 9/13 1ffb2d2  [someone lends]  WE-BUILT-THE-COURTHOUSE-AND-NEVER-ISSUED-A-LOAN -- harvested from ECONOMY round 9. Everything courtless credit needs is LIVE: news walks a real acquaintance graph three hops, memory decays, belonging and deeds are recorded, the purse refuses debt. Nothing in the game ever lends anybody anything, so the enforcement machine has never had a debt to enforce. Let a person or a faction lend you batteries, on a handshake, remembered by the machine that already exists; the debt names its lender (with [debt carried]) and the street finds out if you do not pay.  RESULT: SOMEBODY LENDS YOU BATTERIES NOW, WHICH NOTHING IN THIS GAME DID. Measured first and the row was right: zero hits for lend, loan or borrow in the engine that are not prose, and the only two that read like credit are BARKS -- people in this game TALK about lending and nobody lends. A LOAN IS NOT A NEGATIVE BALANCE, so the purse's rule that balances never go negative was never in the way: the batteries really arrive through the purse's own writer and the obligation sits beside them naming who it is to. WHO LENDS IS NOT A NUMBER I PICKED -- a lender is somebody who gives before you have earned it, and the favour table already says who that is, so 4 outfits lend and 12 will not off the game's own answer, with no rung and no standing floor typed. One battery on a handshake coming back ONE A NIGHT, the shape the night already has for rent, so nothing new was invented to make it bite. IT CAN CLOSE (the account is deleted at zero; interest ships EMPTY because a rate is exactly what makes an interval uncloseable). It rides [debt carried]'s one book as a third kind, so it is named on the nightfall card, dies at the fold and leaves its lender standing with no part of that machinery knowing what a loan is. A NIGHT YOU GO SHORT IS PUBLISHED AS A DEED and really is witnessed and retold. MEASURED RATHER THAN ASSUMED and it corrected me: I wrote that his weight table was empty and waiting on a ruling; it has 83 rows, all derived from the authored quest corpus, and NOT ONE of the five deed kinds the walked city publishes is in it -- so every act this surface publishes weighs nothing. One gap for all five, ROUTED, not patched with a number invented here. records/BOHEMIA_SOMEONE_LENDS_9_13_26.md

- SHIPPED 9/13 f66bae30  [water lifted]  THE-PUMPS-ARE-THE-CITY -- harvested from ECONOMY round 7. The valley floor is about 2,028 feet and Lake Mead hit 1,040.5, so everything Vegas drinks is lifted a thousand feet by 22 vertical pumps and two booster stations. Water is not scarce; PUMPING is. Wire it: a pump station costs power, water costs pumping, thirst costs water, and whoever holds the pumps holds the valley. The cleanest real tie between power, territory and need we will ever get.  RESULT: MEASURED FIRST AND IT WAS WORSE THAN THE ROW SAYS -- the valley drinks 4 L a head a day and NOTHING IN THIS GAME HAD EVER PRODUCED A SINGLE LITRE. The day's produced comes only from the yield table, which is salvage and food, so water was the ONLY good with a need and no way to make it: a one-way countdown with no source, gone in about 65 days, while pump stations, treatment plants and reservoirs sat on the map doing nothing. THE ENERGY IS PHYSICS, NOT A DIAL: his own two measurements give a 987.5 ft lift = 301 m, one litre is one kilogram by definition, E=mgh = 2,952 J = 0.00082 kWh, over 0.75 wire-to-water efficiency = 0.0011 kWh per litre. None of it is his to rule because none of it is a balance choice, and the gate REFUSES any number in the module that is not physics or his measurement. WHAT A STATION DELIVERS IS NOT A DIAL EITHER: a municipal pump station is sized to the population it serves, so a running one covers the need and a dark one covers nothing. *** AND THE FIRST CUT WAS DEAD CODE THAT ONLY MEASURING CAUGHT: *** asking the plant's own cell for power returns id -1 on every water district STRUCTURALLY, because circuits are street runs and a plant is not a street (358 live cells in the valley, every one arterial, freeway or downtown). It would have shipped correct and doing nothing. All six water districts TOUCH a circuit at distance 1, so a plant is served by the street it fronts, which is what a service connection is and what [lights bill] already means. On the real map: the pump station and both treatment plants run, the three reservoirs are dark, and THE ANARCHISTS HOLD THE VALLEY'S WATER. Put the circuits out and the water starts falling again. NOT BUILT ON PURPOSE: the PLAYER's own thirst would be a FIFTH VERB and the four are frozen. Tab: CITY, the nightfall card. records/BOHEMIA_WATER_LIFTED_9_13_26.md

## QUESTS  (the main line and the asking. UNPARKED 9/6.)
MODE: BUILD  *** UNPARKED 9/6 by the coordinator (records/BOHEMIA_RULING_QUESTS_ARE_UNPARKED_9_6_26.md). Paolo parked this 9/4 because he could not walk a quest well enough to judge it; every condition he named has since shipped (the street, the title, the first minute, hostile bodies, the fight where you stand, the room fight, held ground with visible borders, former jobs, standing, outfits, money that bites, the fold, the feed). The fork went to him and he answered VAMILY, which means go, so it is decided under EVERYTHING IS A THUMB and one sentence from him overturns it. ***  RULE 14 (9/13): ship to the alpha; never re-cut the demo; RUN cuts it.
THE SHAPE, DECIDED: the sixty-boss ladder is the MAIN LINE (53 of 60 wired, one verb each, nothing procedural touches it), and everything else is ASKED FOR BY THE SYSTEMS, never written: a person you know, in a place with an owner, wants something they cannot get because of a debt, a bill, a border, an empty shelf or a family that owes. No ask introduces a verb the game does not already do; if it needs a new verb it is a boss, not a quest.
THE ONE RULE THAT MAKES IT WORK: AN ASK THAT CHANGES NOTHING VISIBLE IS NOT AN ASK. The research says generated quests read as filler for exactly one reason -- finishing one changes nothing the player can see -- so every ask must move something visible in a system that already exists (a block changes hands, a debt clears or is called in, a shelf refills, a person moves house, a light comes back on, what people say about you changes). An ask that cannot name what it visibly changes is not offered. Gate that first.
STATE (the lane's own, written 9/6 by the chat holding it): THE GENERATOR EXISTS AND IT RUNS IN THE GAME. engine/bohemia_asks.js turns a running system into somebody asking, and gates/asks_visible_gate.js is 38/0 with the real surface driven. MEASURED IN THE WALKED CITY at the waking block: 61 people on screen, one dark circuit under the player, and the generator hands back a real ask whose visible change is A LIGHT COMES BACK ON. Same result in the demo, because both load the one city file. THE LAW IS IN CODE, NOT IN A COMMENT: offer() throws away every candidate that cannot name a visible change BEFORE anybody is asked anything, and the gate opens each wired change's file and finds its symbol rather than trusting the field, because the cheap way to fake this law is a confident pointer at a system that does not exist. FOUR OF HIS SIX VISIBLE CHANGES ARE WIRED (a block changes hands, a light comes back on, a shelf refills, a rumour turns) AND TWO ARE NOT, stated out loud instead of faked: nothing owns a debt with a name that can be cleared or called in (belonging models debt as a faction WANT, not a balance), and nothing anywhere moves a person from one house to another. Both ship listed and refused, and each turns itself on with no edit once its system is real. Of the four wired, only the grid speaks today: the standing web is wired and quiet because the waking block holds exactly ONE mind, and the shelf has no live per-block ledger reachable from the seam yet. NO ASK USES A VERB THE GAME DOES NOT ALREADY DO (checked against the 14 verbs parsed out of the quest runtime), and no number lives in the table (the gate counts digits). AND THE REAL SURFACE CAUGHT A BUG THE HEADLESS GATE COULD NOT: the first cut asked for people with CELL coordinates when people are keyed by NEIGHBOURHOOD, found nobody in 289 cells, and read as 'the valley is quiet' with every check green, because quiet is a legal answer. Fixed by reusing the city's own ctEveryone(), and the gate now refuses to let un-explained quiet pass. NOT DONE: the ask has no card of its own on screen yet (it is generated and readable, not yet a thing you tap), and nothing applies the change; plan() names the move and never performs it, because the system that owns a change is the one that moves it.
STATE ADDED 9/6 by [spine first]: THE LADDER IS NOW A WALK, AND THE WALK SAYS THE SPINE IS TWO THIRDS EMPTY. His 53 bosses and his 38-edge prerequisite graph are generated into engine/bohemia_ladder_data.js from HIS TWO FILES (nobody typed a boss; the gate re-parses both sources and compares field by field, 0 drifted), and engine/bohemia_ladderwalk.js turns them into the story: who is open to you now, where he stands, who he is, and the verb you take off him. gates/ladder_walk_gate.js is 43/0 with the real surface driven. *** MEASURED, AND IT IS THE ROW'S ANSWER: THE GAME HAS NO WALK AT ALL. *** The fight picks a boss UNIFORMLY AT RANDOM out of everybody you do not already hold (rollBoss: open=BOSSES.filter(not held), then a flat index), so his graph -- approved 8/13, one physical sentence per edge -- has never once decided who you meet, and the fifty-third man can be the first man you meet. The graph is read by two gates and two tools and by NOTHING THE PLAYER TOUCHES. THE THREE COLUMNS, COUNTED: at the start 20 men are open, 20 HAVE A VERB, 2 HAVE A PLACE, and before this round 0 had a person. Across the whole ladder 8 of 53 have a place and 45 do not. THE VERB HALF IS FINISHED WORK AND THE OTHER TWO THIRDS WERE EMPTY. AND THE PLACE WAS ALREADY IN HIS OWN WRITING: his HOLDS column is written in place names (the last kennel, the last clinic, the golf courses, the cemetery, the railyard, the dam, the airfield, the lights on the Strip), so nobody has to decide where a boss stands -- the man who holds the airfield is at the airfield. The walk matches his words against BOH_OVERMAP.DISTRICT, the 79 district kinds this valley really generates, WHOLE WORD ONLY, and carries the matched word out so any placement can be argued with by pointing at two files. NOT ONE PAIRING IS HAND-WRITTEN. THE 45 PLACELESS ARE THE WORK, AND THEY ARE A WORLD JOB, NOT A QUEST ONE: this valley does not build a kennel, a clinic under that name, a forge, a bioreactor, a printing press or a batch plant, so those men have nowhere to stand. A BUG CAUGHT BEFORE IT SHIPPED AND ASSERTED DEAD FOREVER: the first matcher used substrings both ways and confidently placed THE LOCKSMITH at 'blockgen' (block contains lock), THE CHEMIST at 'agents' (reagents contains agents) and THE WALL at 'standing'. Every one would have read as canon, because a place is not the sort of thing a player checks. ALSO MEASURED: the fight publishes bohemiaKeys to the parent every time a key comes off a body and NOTHING OUTSIDE THE FIGHT HAD EVER READ IT; the walk reads it now. NOT DONE: the walk has no card of its own on screen (it is computed and readable, not yet a thing you tap), and the fight still rolls at random because rollBoss belongs to COMBAT and reaching into it from here is the cross-lane write ONE SYSTEM, ONE SESSION forbids. THE OTHER SEVEN OF SIXTY are not written at all and the ladder's own header calls itself a pool to cut from; the real bottleneck is not the missing 7, it is that physics alone opens 20 doors against his 4-to-6 band, which tools/bohemia_ladder_graph.js already measures and which needs a quest gate that is HIS call.
STATE ADDED 9/6 by [main story]: *** THE GAME HAS A MAIN STORY NOW. *** Before this round this repo held 53 fully-produced quest design documents and 27 playable side quests and NOT ONE MAIN-QUEST FILE, which is exactly why the board called it the single largest hole in the game: the story existed only as prose nobody could play. FIVE PLAYABLE FILES NOW CARRY ACT 1 END TO END, and every beat is HIS out of laws/BOHEMIA_STORY_MASTER_7_18_26.md and laws/BOHEMIA_ADDENDUM_ACT1_OPENING_VISION_7_19_26.md: M01 the match-cut open and the night raid (a SIBLING lost, not a parent, his 7/19 reconcile, ending on saving the mother with the assault inferred and never shown), M02 the grief dinner, M03 the burial on the ridge which is also the vista and the title screen, M04 the neighbour's founding, M05 the last hour before the climax. 71 spoken lines, 84 choices, 17 endings, 8 things the game will not let you say, all draft:true. They pass the canon quest bar unchanged (parse, lossless round trip, zero errors AND zero warnings, every reachable path explored, at least one reaching COMPLETE, one clout tag per ending and two distinct tags per quest, a silence option, no stat or karma gate, no hardcoded name anywhere) and the QUEST STUDY LAW bar (16 citations into the 3,672-finding questbook index, id and title verified verbatim). Baked into the workshop AND cut into the demo, so rule 7 is met rather than claimed. *** AND TWO THINGS ARE DELIBERATELY NOT WRITTEN, BECAUSE A MAIN QUEST IS THE MOST EXPENSIVE PLACE IN THE GAME TO INVENT CANON. *** His story master lists the neighbour's first errand under OPEN, EXPLORING TOGETHER (not locked, do not invent unilaterally) and says it is PINNED pending Paolo, so M04 builds the founding CONVERSATION, which is locked and is his, and stops at the exact line where the errand would be named; the neighbour says out loud that he does not have the first job yet, which is the honest version of a ruling nobody has made. And his 7/19 ruling says the Act 1 climax is NOT one fixed scripted scene but a combination of nine approved elements shaped by how the act was played, so M05 names NONE of the nine and picks no climax: it is the door, it records which side of his line the player stood on, and the generator gets to do its job. gates/main_spine_gate.js (49/0) holds both refusals by name, traces every beat back to a phrase in his own locked files, checks all nine elements, and refuses to let the Destroyers be named because his file marks that name PENDING. A BUG CAUGHT IN MY OWN GATE BY A NEGATIVE CONTROL: the pinned-errand check was reading Q.nodes when the parser's field is Q.talks, so it was scanning an empty string and passing no matter what M04 said. Injecting a real errand left it green while the check beside it went red. It now asserts it is reading real text, and four self-tests fire every detector at a string it must catch, because a check nobody has seen fail is a check nobody has tested. It ate 232 lines of another lane's TOWN SIZES and DIR_FAMILY work this round; I caught it by reading git diff --stat before pushing and asking why a commit that ADDS five quests was REMOVING 232 lines, restored both surfaces from origin/main, and spliced my five quests into the BOHEMIA_QUESTS array only, using the tool's own parse_bq. Either that work belongs in the tool or the tool must stop being a whole-block replace.
STATE ADDED 9/7 by [generation handoff]: *** THE FOLD ANSWERS THE QUESTION NOW, AND IT SAYS OUT LOUD THAT IT IS HALF A FOLD. *** THERE ARE TWO FOLDS IN TWO FILES AND NEITHER KNEW THE OTHER EXISTED: foldGeneration in the dynasty engine carries THE LEDGER (standings, territory, builds, economyCapacity, invest, karma, virtues, family, wounds, blindSpot, recordedKnown) and has ZERO callers outside the retired slice; the walked city's ctFold carries THE MEMORY and returned three bare numbers that appeared in exactly two places in the file, where they were computed and where they were returned. NOTHING CONSUMED THEM. Those two numbers are what the heir's first hour is made of. engine/bohemia_fold.js COMPOSES the two rather than replacing either, because both belong to other systems, and gates/fold_runtime_gate.js is 35/0 with the real surface driven. THE CARRY LIST IS THE STUDY'S, thirteen fields with the measured reason each one says what it says, and the gate traces every field back to records/BOHEMIA_DYNASTY_DAY_13_WHAT_CARRIES_AND_WHAT_MUST_DIE so a field added by hand goes red. WHAT YOU KEPT COMES FIRST and what died follows, because losses are felt harder than equal gains and the order is the design. DEBT IS THE ONE THING THAT DIES: a child is not liable for a parent's unsecured debts, so you inherit less and you inherit the people he owed, still standing there, which is a standing-web query rather than a purse line. *** AND IT INVENTS NO DECAY. *** EIGHT OF THE THIRTEEN FIELDS HAVE NO RULED RATE (territory, builds, economyCapacity, invest, karma, virtues, family, wounds) and they are LISTED as unruled and carry whole rather than getting a number I picked; the gate fails on any fraction in the module's logic. The one rate this game has, STANDING_DECAY_TO_NEUTRAL at 0.25, is still the engine's and this round did not move it: the study measured real status persistence at about 0.79, so 0.75 is very nearly right AND IT IS ON THE WRONG FIELD, because it is the only field that decays while wealth, which really persists at 0.3 to 0.4, carries whole forever. Reported, not changed: how his hundred years feel is a ruling. MEASURED ON THE REAL SURFACE: the dynasty engine is genuinely not in the walked city (zero hits for foldGeneration), so the beat returns missing:'ledger' every time instead of a confident half, and a negative control proves the check bites if it ever claims to be whole. NOT DONE ON PURPOSE: no scene. The row says the beat itself and canon is his, and who dies at the end of act one is PARKED on Paolo's own words, so this is the machine for the moment and not one line of its story.
STATE ADDED 9/7 by [distance shown]: *** A JOB SAYS HOW LONG IT IS BEFORE YOU TAKE IT. *** The row's arithmetic held and is re-derived by the gate from the surface's own measured constants: nine metres a minute, a block is 384 m, so five blocks is the row's 1.9 km and about 211 minutes ONE WAY, seven hours there and back, and a sixteen-hour day walks the row's 8.6 km across a 9.2 km valley. MEASURED LIVE ON DAY ONE: the required role casts SIX blocks out, which is 256 minutes each way and 512 of the day's 960 minutes of light spent walking, and until this round nothing said a word about it. Now the wake card reads THE METER READER / nobody has picked it up yet / "about 4 and a half hours on foot, about 8 and a half hours there and back" / GET UP. *** DISCLOSURE, NOT RELOCATION: nothing moved, nothing shortened, MAP LAW untouched, and the gate asserts the module writes into no other system and never touches a block, a layout or a person. *** THE ONE THRESHOLD IS HIS OWN, out of the row: a day there and back is the reachable set, from the City of London's market isochrone. Anything finer would be a number nobody ruled, so the file states the minutes and answers exactly one yes-or-no question; the gate NAMES EVERY NUMBER in the code with what it is for and fails on any it cannot account for. *** AND THE ROUND'S REAL LESSON, WHICH IS A MISTAKE I MADE AND CAUGHT: my first cut hung the line on ctAddress(), which reads beautifully and is ON THE WRONG SIDE OF THE DECISION. *** The address needs the day cast, the cast only exists after DQ.openDay, and openDay only runs when he ACCEPTS, so that version told him the walk was seven hours AFTER he had agreed to it, which is the exact failure this row exists to end, rebuilt inside the fix for it. It now rides the OFFER, and the gate demands the sentence exist while OFFER_TAKEN is still false. Proven on the workshop AND the demo, both showing it before acceptance. The offer measures with the SAME castAddresses call the address line uses, deliberately, so one person can never be quoted two different distances.
STATE ADDED 9/7 by [ten openings]: *** ACT ONE HAS SEVEN ASKS THAT DID NOT EXIST, AND THEY ALL WORK THE SAME WAY. He locked it 7/19: the ten opening options not used in the cold open become the first act-one quests, nothing is wasted. Seven of the ten had never been written anywhere, fifteen rounds later. They exist now as playable .bq: THE KILLING SUMMER, THE ELDER'S ACCIDENT, THE FACTION THAT DIED, THE WEDDING THAT BURNED, THE DRY TAPS, THE FIRST HARVEST, THE TWO FAMILIES. Six quote his own sentence from the locked element set in their header. THE TWO FAMILIES has nothing behind it but the name, and its header says that in the first line so nobody later reads an attempt as canon. EVERY ONE IS THE SAME SHAPE, which is the [check the claim] row's shape: somebody makes a flat calm specific claim, nothing in their face is a tell (118 of 158 measured deception cues mean nothing and people catch a lie 47% of the time, so THE LIAR IS BELIEVED), and the only way to check it is to GO TO THE THING and look. Count the crates in the store. Read the drag marks in the ground where the fence posts were. Stand where he landed. No highlighted option, no roll, no stat gate anywhere in the seven. What changes is always something you can watch happen: a light, a shelf, a tap, who is standing on a piece of ground, what the block says about you. TWO CONTENT BUGS THE PAY GATE CAUGHT AND I FIXED THE FILES RATHER THAN THE GATE: A02 had a COMPLETE ending that paid nothing (the quiet ending is paid in the box, resources, and the loud ones in clout: one currency per ending, what you spent to get it is the difference), and A04 called going back to the table after finding a hole in the perimeter a COMPLETE. Walking away from a hole you found is not a completion; it is a FAIL and it pays nothing. ALSO REPAIRED, in this lane, on the way: gates/dayloop_gate.js was 57/2 red ON MAIN before I touched anything, both fails hardcoded quest sentences that no .bq has said since WORDS rewrote day one. The check is literally named 'quotes the quest, not me', so it reads the line out of the parsed file now instead of remembering it. Retyping the new sentence would have fixed the red and rebuilt the same trap. 59/0, negative-controlled both ways. ***
STATE ADDED 9/11 by [designs playable]: *** THE PROSE DESIGNS ARE NOT A BACKLOG, THEY ARE A BUILD ORDER, AND THEY EXPIRE. Designs 001, 002 and 013 were finished production documents from 7/10 with cast, node trees, branch patterns and fold consequences, and nobody could play a word of them. They are playable now. THE FINDING FOR WHOEVER CONVERTS THE NEXT ONES: a straight transcription DOES NOT PARSE, because the designs predate three laws. They gate their best lines on skill levels ([MEDICINE], [TRADES], [BARTER], [READ], [INTIMIDATE]) and the parser bans stat gates outright; they name six people and the name law forbids it; they pay in caps and caps are dead. Every one of those converts to something BETTER rather than something less: a skill gate becomes a thing you learn by GOING AND LOOKING, which is what the designs were reaching for anyway (002's own Landsmeet note says evidence wins the room and overreach loses it, it just asked for it with a number), and a proper name becomes a @ROLE cast at runtime. THE RULE THIS LANE IS NOW APPLYING TO ALL OF THEM: resolve every disagreement in favour of the law AND WRITE IT INTO THE HEAD OF THE FILE in plain words, so the next chat can see in twenty lines what was changed and why, and nobody ever silently loses a design decision. 53 prose designs remain unconverted (56 in quests/, three of them now have a machine layer). The three hardest parts of each one are the skill gates, the names and the money, and all three now have a worked answer. ***
STATE ADDED 9/11 by [haggling works]: *** A JOB SAYS WHAT IT PAYS NOW, AND YOU CAN ARGUE ABOUT IT. The offer card had a title, a 'nobody has picked it up yet', and a length. It had no price. That was the real hole in this row and it was hiding under the word haggle. THE FINDING FOR ANY LANE COPYING A MECHANIC OUT OF A GAME HE NAMED: take the SHAPE and leave the numbers, and say in the file which is which. The campaign game's haggle is a random 3-6 annoyance against a threshold of 9. The shape ('safe once, risky twice, nearly impossible three times') is worth having and the numbers are somebody else's economy. Ours gets the same shape from a fact instead of a dial: the count of times you opened your mouth, and a menu with exactly two things on it. No threshold to tune, nothing random, and the player is warned in words before the ask that costs them, which is also what our own [check the claim] rule demands (nothing hidden decides an outcome). AND EVERYTHING COSTS ONE FORCED THE BEST PART: you cannot ask for two, so asking for more had to mean asking for a different SHAPE of the one, which is a better mechanic than a bigger number and is the study's own 'payment shapes' finding. Reuse: the standing mark is a deed row handed to the deed ledger, so no new standing system exists. ***
STATE ADDED 9/12 by [edit quests]: *** HE CAN DIRECT A QUEST NOW, AND THE THING THAT UNLOCKED IT WAS NOT A FEATURE. The DIRECT tab could already show a quest, move a row, delete a row and retarget a choice. What it could not do was touch the half that decides how a quest ENDS, and it could not play one. BOTH WERE DOWNSTREAM OF ONE HONEST DECISION MADE ON 8/12: the row reader was deliberately lossy, and its own comment said pretending to edit something that does not round-trip would be worse than not showing it. That was right. SO THE UNLOCK WAS MAKING THE ROWS LOSSLESS -- one row per line of his file, each carrying its own line -- and then every verb the law asks for is just allowed. THE FINDING FOR ANY LANE BUILDING AN EDITOR OVER CANON: a lossy view is not a small version of an editor, it is a ceiling on one, and you pay for it later in features you cannot build. AND THE CHECK THAT MATTERS IS NOT VALIDITY, IT IS MEANING: deleting COMPLETE from a stage leaves perfectly legal .bq, so a rebuilder that loses meaning without losing legality hands his file back looking fine and playing differently. The gate parses the original and the rebuild and compares what they mean. The alpha now carries each quest's SOURCE (397 KB, against 314 KB for the generated rows it replaced) plus the real parser and the real runtime (32 KB), which is what makes PLAY the thing he built rather than a preview of a plan. ***
STATE ADDED 9/12 by [check the claim]: *** THE VALLEY CAN SAY SOMETHING THAT IS NOT TRUE NOW, AND THE ONLY INSTRUMENT FOR SETTLING IT IS YOUR LEGS. engine/bohemia_claims.js reads the SAME snapshot bohemia_asks.js reads and asks a different question of it: not what does this person want, but what does this person SAY. A claim that cannot be checked by walking somewhere is refused before a word is put in anybody's mouth, which is its sibling's admission ticket applied to a different problem. THE HARD PART WAS REFUSING THE OBVIOUS FEATURE: every game makes the liar fidget, and that is the 118 measured cues that mean nothing rebuilt as a mechanic. If manner predicts, nobody ever walks and the row is dead. So manner is a hash of the PLACE and the gate proves it cannot carry truth, over 480 claims. *** AND THE BIGGEST FINDING IS ABOUT THE GRID, NOT ABOUT CLAIMS: POWER.at ANSWERS {live:false,id:-1} FOR EVERY COORDINATE IN EXISTENCE. *** It never says it does not know, so any system that trusts it will confidently report a place that does not exist as dark. id:-1 is its own marker for no circuit. Measured by asking it about NaN and about a cell a million away. AND THERE IS NO REAL CIRCUIT ANYWHERE THE PLAYER CAN REACH: 120,801 cells probed, zero. So the one live ASK the game produces today -- light_comes_back, which [asks exist] shipped 9/6 -- is about a circuit that does not exist, and its `about` field is literally -1. That is this lane's next row, not this one's, because fixing it here would take a shipped row's own ship test red inside a different job. ***
STATE ADDED 9/13 by [company in asks]: *** YOU HAVE PEOPLE NOW, AND NOBODY KEEPS A LIST OF THEM. A bond earned by finishing a job makes that person yours, by name, and deleting the bond un-makes it in the same instant. THE FINDING FOR ANY LANE ASKED FOR A ROSTER, A PARTY, A CREW OR A COMPANY: do not build the list. A list is a thing somebody has to maintain and the moment it exists it can disagree with the world. Compute membership from the records the world already keeps, every call, and the disagreement becomes impossible rather than unlikely. THREE LEDGERS ALREADY RECORD A PERSON AND THE PLAYER TOGETHER -- a bond (quest runtime), a witness (deed ledger), a roof (century household stamp) -- and the third one is a COUNT PER ACT that can never name anybody. That limit is enforced in code so a number can never become a face. AND THE TRAP THE LIVE DRIVE FOUND, WHICH ANY SEAM READING A CAST WILL HIT: ctDayCast() goes null the moment a quest is done, and the bond is earned AT the ending -- so at the exact instant somebody becomes yours, the only thing that can turn their role into a person disappears. Read the recorded cast, not the function. ITS LIMIT, STATED: that record is keyed on quest and day, so a bond from an earlier day loses its cast when a new one is rung. Naming somebody for good needs a record that outlives a day and that is a row somebody has to build. ***
STATE ADDED 9/6 by [jobs pay]: *** A JOB PAYS NOW. *** The row's measurement held: across every playable quest there were 786 @DO calls and @DO pay appeared ZERO TIMES. The verb has existed since 8/11 on HIS OWN RULING ("whatever currency the quest decides to give"), the purse was built, the payday bridge was built, payForQuest is genuinely called on the walked surface with a live quest runtime, and not one job in the valley paid a thing because the last line was never typed. 66 PAY LINES NOW, ACROSS 20 QUESTS, and the currency of each is read out of that quest's own words: the meter reader pays electricity because the whole quest is the current somebody is drinking off the block's feed; the standing bounty pays resources because its own line is "clean conscience, full satchel"; the broadcast at three pays clout because nobody pays for it and what it buys is that forty people heard it from you. EVERY AMOUNT IS ONE and the gate fails on any other number (EVERYTHING COSTS ONE, and the amounts are his to tune). Only a COMPLETE ending pays; a FAIL pays nothing, so the reward can never become an attendance prize. *** AND TWELVE QUESTS PAY NOTHING ON PURPOSE, EACH WITH A WRITTEN REASON IN ITS OWN FILE. *** Nobody pays you for burying your sibling; the doctor's own words are "no charge, no interest, no paper"; the forgiveness quest says cheap forgiveness is just a debt paid on time. A game where everything pays is a game where nothing means anything, so the gate demands that an unpaid quest SAY WHY, which turns the difference between a deliberate refusal and an oversight into something a machine can tell apart. THE ROW'S OWN SHIP TEST IS MET ON THE REAL SURFACE: the gate opens the game, walks the meter reader to COMPLETE inside the frame the player looks at, hands it to the real payday bridge, and the purse goes from electricity 0 to electricity 1. gates/the_job_pays_gate.js is 75/0. A THIRD COPY OF THE QUESTS FOUND, AND IT HAD GONE STALE: the city PLAYS from its own DEMO_BQ block, separate from quests/bq and from the alpha's BOHEMIA_QUESTS, and tools/bohemia_city_dayloop_patch.py REFUSES TO REFRESH IT ("the installed clock was not found after the block, so replacing would eat somebody else's code"). VERIFIED PRE-EXISTING by stashing my whole tree and re-running on clean main: same refusal, so no lane has been able to refresh the city's playing copy for some time and nobody noticed. I refreshed it surgically instead (that one object, 2 lines of diff, every other byte untouched) and flagged the tool. The tool's refusal is GOOD behaviour, not a bug; what is broken is the anchor it looks for. FOR THE PLUMBER.

MODE: BUILD  RULE 14 (9/13): ship to the alpha; never re-cut the demo; RUN cuts it.
STATE: 65 approved sounds, 185 variants. EVERY SONG PLAYS THE INSTRUMENT ITS OWN ROW NAMES SINCE 9/13: 109 of 142 songs named a lead voice scheduled ZERO times and the tune was one bare triangle wave on 135 of 142, so 120 distinct cooked voices were replaced at play time by one sound; the melody branch reached the named lead only for mel='hymn' or a lead called 'bell'. Fixed as a mechanism (the row is his content) plus a FLOOR, because synthV's 581 branches ended in nothing and an unknown voice name was silence. THE SHAPE OF THE STREET IS MEASURED END TO END: the accent at 0.0s, the tune at 8.0s, the kit at 16.0s, so the melody is already playing when the beat arrives on all 142 songs -- and a measurement that said otherwise had been taken in the MUSIC tab, where the kit is never held on purpose. The dusk/dawn pool is 4 songs, up from 2. Two loud defects are named and NOT re-balanced (THE GAPS IN THE HYMNAL 25.6x the median, CANON; MENU - LIGHTS ACROSS THE VALLEY 1.064 peak, BURIED). AND THE 120 BPM CHECKER IS TRUSTWORTHY NOW: BEAT FIRST had four faults and the beat had none, so it was a coin flip deciding every lane's ship; no playStep on either tree comes near the 125ms budget and the median cost got cheaper. UNHEARD BY A PERSON: the full ramp on a phone -- the rest, then bass and accent, then the tune, then the beat at sixteen, about 32 seconds of shape, every number of it measured and none of it listened to. WHAT THE GAME PLAYS IS NOW A MEASUREMENT, NOT A GREP: a full drive of the walked surface produces THIRTEEN of the 65, and CORRECTED 9/15: that claim was FALSE and this lane wrote it. The shipped step bank held THREE surfaces (dirt, asphalt, gravel) while the bank he approved held six, so every concrete and every sand footfall played the DIRT sample through a fallback -- a sidewalk has been telling his ears it is a dirt path. The 9/5 round measured the CITY'S CLASSIFIER, which really does name five, and never checked that the shell could play them: A CENSUS OF WHAT IS ASKED FOR IS NOT A CENSUS OF WHAT CAN SOUND. Fixed 9/15, five surfaces now, every variant copied out of his own approved bank. THE DESERT SOUNDS LIKE THE DESERT since 9/5: it was playing the suburban lawn footstep because the pool table sends every unnamed ground to the YARD pool, so step_sand was approved 8/12 and had never once played. step_wood is unreachable with a written reason (no wooden ground exists in the valley, measured across 18 districts). THE END OF A DAY MAKES A SOUND SINCE 9/5: it was completely silent when the clock ran the light out, while choosing to sleep had had a sound since 8/22, with the audio engine proved running for every sample; the other 56 each carry a written reason the drive cannot reach them. LIGHT=TERRITORY IS AUDIBLE: a live circuit hums and a dead block never does, the distance is the grid's own answer (on it, next street, two streets, then nothing), and a dead street still has wind and air. The bed is also the PLACE: 79 districts in four kinds (machine 25, lit 19, open 18, lived 17), and the audible half is HOW OFTEN it speaks -- a lit block every 25-60s, open desert every 60-130s. The game also has a PULSE from the tap -- one looping audio-thread buffer at 120 BPM, quieter than a footstep -- covering the ten seconds of silence while the city loads, and the first song takes the beat off it. The walked city NOW reports where you are every four seconds, so the ambience bed runs, occlusion has a listener, the music phase follows the clock and the hour chime strikes -- all four measured on the real surface, all four dark for three weeks before 9/5. Also fixed 9/5: every footstep in the valley was the dirt one (the classifier read two fields a city cell does not have; 6,561 of 6,561 cells came back 'dirt'). Unverified: where the music engine module lives (not in engine/).

- OPEN  [quiet floor]  THERE-IS-NO-SILENT-OUTDOORS -- EYES E12's school round changed the fix: real quiet has a FLOOR (wilderness 30-40 dBA and seldom below, a quiet suburb 45-50, a city 60-70, night about 7 dB under day), so a dead city is only five to seven decibels quieter than a live one, and a valley that goes to nothing when the player stops walking is not "quiet", it is a bug. Give the night its floor: the cooked bed, called, at the wilderness level, with the seven-decibel drop at night. Pairs with EYES E12 round two.

NOTES ARE RULINGS: his 8/2 word stands. Tab: MUSIC. (coordinator 9/13)

## LIFE + CITY  (03. the city-builder tab and the aerial view; the buildings, the housing, the century)
MODE: BUILD  RULE 14 (9/13): ship to the alpha; never re-cut the demo; RUN cuts it.
STATE: build verbs and a picker EXIST in the aerial tab (engine/bohemia_cityedit.js, cityTapPlot) and the panel is PROVEN under a thumb on an iPhone profile (9/5). Placed buildings now PRODUCE on the wake beat: engine/bohemia_production.js walks the delta and calls the purse's produce(), 59 buildable types carry a row, resources 0 -> 1 across a night on the walked surface, and the morning card says so (9/5). BUILDING COSTS ONE BATTERY out of the same pocket the shop charges, checked before the tap and refused in words when he is broke, proven on the walked surface and in the cut demo (9/5). THE BUILDER REACHES THE STREET: a BUILD HERE chip on the walked surface opens the same panel for the cell under his feet, same verbs, same price, proven on the walked surface and in the cut demo (9/5). CORRECTED 9/5, measured not assumed: the builder ALWAYS reached the demo -- the earlier "not in the demo" note was grepping BOHEMIA_RUN_CURRENT.html, which is not the file the demo builds its city from. SOMEBODY LIVES IN WHAT YOU BUILT: a placed home houses one household at the population module's own HOUSEHOLD_MEAN, residents are CAPPED BY THE VALLEY because the 7/29 food ceiling is locked and housing does not create people, the plot says who is in it (scoped PLOT for his own, BLOCK for generated ground) and the morning card carries the number; proven on the walked surface and in the cut demo (9/5). THE CITY IS THE LONG MEMORY: engine/bohemia_century.js is a per-act LEDGER of what each generation built and tore down (not a view over the delta, so act 2 knocking down act 1's work does not erase act 1), it persists through the real save and restore, and the reckoning card carries the generation's tally; TIERS ships EMPTY and tierOf() says NO_RULING by name, because what a poor city and a rebuilt city ARE is [PENDING Paolo] (9/5). The record defaults to act 1 and offers one setter for the fold, which is the quests queue's PARKED [generation handoff]. THE VALLEY TALKS ON THE CITY PHONE: engine/bohemia_feedstream.js is ONE stream with the law's three sources -- the deed ledger (quoting the quest's own @LOG line), the world (lights, prices, faction seats, new builds, each spoken only when it MOVES), and ambient life keyed off what exists rather than a fixed list -- and the UI surface now reads only it; the two stopgaps it was carrying are retired and a gate leg proves none of their lines can reach the panel (9/5). First caller BohemiaTowns has ever had on the walked surface: 14 faction seats off the graph. THE FIGHT CAN STAND ON THE CITY: engine/bohemia_combatfloor.js hands COMBAT a drawable floor -- the walked surface registers its OWN renderCity as the painter, so the floor IS the city at those coordinates and not a second render, the camera is put back exactly, plan() answers where the blocks are with no pixels drawn, and a tile at or above the 112px sprite is REFUSED because the player stays the same size (9/4 law 3b). A figure is 6.22 blocks wide at the city's own tile. Cells carry scope:"block" -- a cell here is a district, not a house (9/5). NOT built: what you place is not yet standing in the art when you walk to it ([buildings appear]). KNOWN: the demo is ONE DAY (CT_DEMO_DAYS=1) so no second wake beat exists there. Unverified: which building types make electricity or clout instead of resources is [PENDING Paolo].

- SHIPPED 9/15 city-1eztay  [lot lattice]  WHERE-A-STEP-MAY-LAND -- *** PAOLO 9/15, rule 16. *** THE ROW'S PREMISE WAS WRONG AND SO WAS RUN'S COPY OF IT: both say "a lot is FN=32 fine cells". FN IS 128, and the 7/6 VALLEY SCALE LAW calls one FN cell A NEIGHBOURHOOD, NOT A LOT, in those words -- the 32 is a dead comment left over from before the 7/30 relock, and taken literally a step would have been 96 m, a city block. THE GAME ALREADY HAD A LOT AND NOBODY LOOKED: engine/bohemia_suburb.js packs houses every widest-model-plus-gap = 24 fine cells = 18 m. LOT_FINE IS THAT NUMBER, read out of the generator by the gate every run, so the law's "a second copy anywhere is the bug" has a machine holding it. One step = 18 m = 2 minutes at the reach module's own walking speed (derived, never typed); travel gets 24x faster; a body is half a lot tall, one number, his to correct. MEASURED FROM THE DEMO'S DOOR WITH THE ONE DRIVER, five places, 288 m square each, on the game's own cellAt().walk: a step landing on LOT CORNERS puts a suburb at 45% standable with a 23% biggest island -- the obvious lattice cuts the world into pieces -- so the landing is A CELL YOU CAN STAND ON, road first (Paolo 8/1 NO DISTRICT IS A PRISON, so the lattice node and the drop-in's arrival are the same cell by construction). RESULT, AND IT SURPRISED ME: THE LOT LATTICE IS MORE CONNECTED THAN THE FINE ONE IN EVERY DISTRICT -- door 83.5% -> 94.1%, commercial 85.8% -> 99.2%, gated 81.3% -> 98.8% -- because a lot step crosses a one-cell fence gap a fine step walks around. A step is refused unless a body can walk between two lots' landings WITHOUT LEAVING THEM, so a body never cuts the corner of a house. SHIPPED: engine/bohemia_lattice.js (canon-declared the day it was written), inlined in the walked city and published as __proof.lattice for RUN/COMBAT/CHARACTER to read, latCtx() built once, the city drop-in arriving on a node, tools/bohemia_lot_lattice_probe.js, gate WHERE A STEP MAY LAND 36/0, mutation-tested three ways. Rule 14 and one-system-one-session both say the door is RUN's ([spawn home] is claimed): the lattice snaps TRAVEL and leaves the door alone, and E1 goes red if anybody starts snapping it. FOR RUN: THE PLAYER STILL MOVES ONE FINE CELL A PRESS -- [step is a house] is yours and this is the ground under it; read BOH_LATTICE, do not write a second constant. FOR RUN AND EYES, MEASURED WHILE CHASING THE ARRIVAL: THERE IS NO DROP IN CONTROL IN THE DEMO'S DOM AT ALL (#modechip does not exist; a sweep of every element in city mode matching DROP/MAP/GO/ENTER/LAND finds two zero-size labels), so the only city-to-street arrival that runs is the boot -- his break list says the button 'exists' and it does not. records/BOHEMIA_WHERE_A_STEP_MAY_LAND_9_15_26.md -- *** PAOLO 9/15, rule 16. *** FIRST LINE OF THIS LANE. TEN ROUNDS. Rounds 8-10 went BEYOND the ship test and bought a CROWD as well, 0 -> 13 of 16. HONESTLY LEFT OPEN, because a half-done row marked shipped is worse than an open one: THREE WALKS IN SIXTEEN still find only the floor, and the probe says all three are the same cause as the eight before them -- a crowd big enough, standing behind him. Whoever takes that next should start from the probe method in records/BOHEMIA_IN_FRONT_OF_HIM_IS_NOT_ON_HIS_WAY_9_12_26.md, not from a theory. -- ROUND 10 SHIPPED: 13 OF 16 WALKS NOW MEET A CROWD (8 -> 13), AND THE ROUND OPENED BY BEING WRONG ABOUT WHY. *** I assumed the eight misses were crowds too small. A probe that recovered the knot from public state on every sample said the opposite and it was not close: of the 8 misses, crowd never big enough 0, big enough but BEHIND 8, big enough and ahead 0. Every miss had 12 or 13 bodies in it against a threshold of 7 -- NOT ONE WALK EVER FAILED FOR WANT OF PEOPLE. Round 8 had bought the crowd and never spent it: the spot was the best frontage in ANY BEARING and a straight walk has even odds. FIRST CUT WAS A HALF-PLANE AND BOUGHT ONE WALK (8 -> 9, noise). The walk that went the other way named the cause: crowd ahead for 43 of 50 samples, peak seen 1. Suspected a CARROT (the field re-placing it ahead each rebuild) and MEASURED IT DEAD -- the anchor moved 0 or 1 times in a whole walk, the crowd stands still, round 9 is doing its job. What the run did show, on four misses with hundreds of cells of travel each: CLOSEST HE EVER GOT 13, 14, 15, 15, against a SEE_RANGE of 9. Walk 1 went 398 cells dead straight and passed the crowd at 14. A HALF-PLANE IS 180 DEGREES WIDE, so a crowd 80 degrees off his line is "ahead" and he walks right past it. SHIPPED: the crowd must stand forward of him AND within one SEE_RANGE of the LINE he is walking -- perpendicular offset, and SEE is not a tuning constant but the definition of the question (when he draws level with it, it has to be close enough to see). A filter, not a weight, so "the best forecourt" stays exactly as true; falls back to round 8's rule when there is no heading or no frontage on his line. The heading is READ, not invented: the surface stores no facing, and two rebuilds apart IS the heading. NOT round 9's mirage -- nothing changes about when a field is discarded, only which way the NEXT crowd forms. Floor unmoved at 16 of 16. Gate 9/0, B1 ratcheted 6 -> 11, and the two mutations RE-MEASURED THE HISTORY from the other direction: drop the perpendicular rule and the walk reads 9 of 16, drop the heading and it reads 8. STANDING NOTE: IN FRONT OF HIM AND ON HIS WAY ARE NOT THE SAME THING, AND THE FIRST IS WORTH ALMOST NOTHING. records/BOHEMIA_IN_FRONT_OF_HIM_IS_NOT_ON_HIS_WAY_9_12_26.md. ROUND 9 BEFORE IT: HALF OF ALL WALKS NOW MEET A CROWD (5 of 16 -> 8 OF 16). The eleven missing walks had nothing to do with crowds: the field rebuilt when he crossed a 512-CELL GRID LINE, so a 400-step walk got one or two chances however far it went. Anchoring the rebuild on HIS OWN POSITION was tried first and measured WORSE (4 of 16) -- every sixty cells it threw away the crowd he might have been walking toward, so he was chasing a mirage. ANCHORED ON THE CROWD, a crowd he is approaching is never discarded. Gate ratcheted from 3 to 6 so this cannot slip back. records/BOHEMIA_A_CROWD_IS_NOT_ONE_CROWD_9_12_26.md. ROUND 8 BEFORE IT: THE PEAKS. Round 7 built the floor (empty standings 116 of 120 -> 65, every walk meets somebody) but the most he ever saw AT ONCE was 2. MEASURED: the crowds were already built -- standing on residential ground the peak was 2, standing ON a gathering place it was 17, 14, 11, 10, 9 -- HE JUST NEVER ENDS UP ON ONE. Two cuts then failed the same way and A WALK caught both (filling the nearest real place doubled the crowd at a place from 17 to 37 and a walk still met 0 crowds in 16). SHIPPED: the crowd forms on the best frontage within TWO SCREENFULS of him, beyond his own sight, sized at the module's own HEADS.cluster and capped at half the borrowed people so the floor survives. AFTER: walks that met a crowd 0 of 16 -> 5 OF 16, biggest group seen 3 -> 14, every walk still meets somebody, empty standings unchanged. gates/a_crowd_to_walk_into_gate.js 7/0 AND IT WALKS, mutation-tested two ways. records/BOHEMIA_A_CROWD_TO_WALK_INTO_9_12_26.md. STILL OPEN: the other eleven walks. -- dispatch item 5, "dead is not the default"; the number is his, the mechanism (a default that is not zero) is ours *** DECIDED 9/7 BY THE COORDINATOR (a number is never his question): the ship test is met by DENSITY, not headcount. The valley keeps its canon count; the mechanism places people where the player IS (LIFE+CITY already shipped "a bearing is a place" and "the day was what emptied the street"), so the ~655 needed within meeting range are the people whose day brings them there, spawned near and released far, which is how every open world on earth fakes a city. Default: the street a player is on is never empty in daylight. Unblocked; build it. ***

THE ROW'S THREE MUST-NOTS EACH HELD ON THE GLASS: not a heal button (1 hp from 0, no button anywhere), not invulnerable (one volley puts her back down, downed not dead), no control surface (of 101 pressable things, ZERO order her about -- the 8/31 no-order-menu law). She goes down through the SHIPPED FIRE and not a flag, and nothing stands her up alone over 30 turns with you nine tiles away, which is what makes it BEING MISSED rather than a wait (study day 4: permadeath does not create attachment, it cashes in a bond that already exists). TWO INSTRUMENT BUGS IN MY OWN GATE: the staging was backwards (acq=0 against acquired()'s acq>=ACQ_TURNS, 400 volleys landed nothing), and MUTATION A ESCAPED -- deleting allyPickup() from the footfall left it GREEN because the arm called the function directly. An arm that calls the function proves the function works, not that the game reaches it. It drives the real worldShift walk now; that mutation is 3 reds.
