- OPEN  [day one]  A-DAY-YOU-COULD-DESCRIBE -- one full in-game day in the demo, from waking to the bed, that a player could describe to somebody else afterwards without using a single one of our words. Walk it, write what happens, and name the parts that are still nothing.
- OPEN  [five minutes]  A-UNIT-OF-PLAY-THAT-FITS-A-BUS-RIDE -- harvested from DYNASTY's hundred-hour round (records/BOHEMIA_HARVEST_TWO_A_HUNDRED_HOURS_IN_FIVE_MINUTE_BITES_9_6_26.md). The measured reality: a mobile session is 5 to 6 minutes, four times a day, and 96% of players are gone by day seven. We are building a hundred-hour game for that person. Not an argument against the hundred hours -- it is the point -- but it decides the SHAPE: build a unit of play that fits five minutes and is worth something finished (a job done, a debt paid, a block walked, one fight), with a real stopping point at the end of it rather than a thing abandoned half-done. The save is already hard; this is about the shape of the play, not the save.
- OPEN  [a shift]  A-SHIFT-IS-THE-FIVE-MINUTE-UNIT -- with WORLD [a days work] and RUN [five minutes]: a shift is the piece of play that fits a bus ride. It starts, it ends, and there is a battery at the end of it. Somebody is expecting you at a place at a time; you go, you do it, you get paid, you can put the phone down. That is the smallest complete thing this game can offer, and it does not exist.
- OPEN  [a day is]  THE-DAY-IS-A-DIAL-AND-FIFTEEN-MINUTES-IS-THE-DEFAULT -- the craft's answer is that a full day in about fifteen real minutes makes a day feel like a decision (the best farm-life game does this and copied a brisker ancestor on purpose), while real-time days let a player leave without fear. We need both: a day short enough to decide, a session that never punishes leaving. Put day length on a dial in DEMO SETTINGS, default fifteen real minutes, so a shift (RUN [a shift]) is a third of a day. Needs PLUMBER [clock math] for the current number.
- OPEN  [map on beat]  DOES-THE-CITY-MARKER-OBEY-THE-120-BPM-REQUEST-LAW -- the 7/6 lock says movement is a REQUEST executed on the 500 ms beat, "a world law, not a mode rule", on foot AND in city mode. On foot it holds (BEAT is 500 in both shipped files). In city mode no beat hook on the map marker is visible to a text search, which proves nothing (a run-time hook is invisible to grep). [lock them] stays held behind it)  [lock them]  ONE-TAP-LOCKS-A-PERSON-YOU-WILL-NOT-LOSE -- Paolo 9/11: "there has to be a way to make it easy to lock characters they don't want to die, companions, people in your company, not endgame bullshit." One tap on the person, from the first hour, no gate, no rare item. A locked person is never even close: when they would fall they go down for the long timer. It costs something real so it is a choice: they are carried when they fall (the party slows), their injuries cost, and the world saw them fall (a scar in the standing web). Numbers are the coordinator's, the shape is his. *** ARMED 9/12 by the coordinator, from the craft: in the games that made companions unkillable, the weight of a fall is WHAT YOU LOSE WHILE THEY ARE DOWN -- the thing they were giving you (their perk, their turn in the fight, their share of the carry) goes with them for the whole timer, so protecting them still matters without a death. So a locked person who falls costs you exactly their contribution for a week, a season or a year (PEOPLE [down not dead] shipped those three lengths off his own words), plus the carry and the treatment. Nothing else needs inventing. ***

So a player who wronged somebody in hour two can never make it right, and standing is a one-way ratchet toward being hated -- and generation three is the ANGEL, who cannot forgive anything. Build the other half: a deed can be settled, paid off, forgiven or spared, the web learns it happened, and the person who was wronged is the one who decides. Top of this lane.

- OPEN  [weights shape]  WHAT-PEOPLE-REPEAT-ABOUT-YOU -- RULED by the coordinator 9/5 (correct-after, from the research, Paolo may overrule): people repeat a HANDFUL of things about a stranger and nothing else: did you hurt someone, did you steal, did you help someone who needed it, did you keep your word, did you pay what you owed. Everything else in the 82-row deed table weighs near zero. A betrayal is worth about five kindnesses (negativity bias is the best-replicated finding in the field), it is remembered longer, and it travels further. Weight the table to that shape, then watch the WHO WILL VOUCH panel light up. (pending from PEOPLE, 9/5)

The city already gossips properly: hop counts, hearsay decay, a quiet deed dies with its witness while a notorious one is what your child gets judged for. In 1,669 lines it has passed on news about somebody other than the player exactly ONCE, and it has never once been WRONG. We even wrote the line where a stranger says "probably the wrong version" and there has never been a wrong version. Make rumours travel about other people, and make them distort as they go.
- OPEN  [creditor stands]  YOU-DO-NOT-INHERIT-A-BILL-YOU-INHERIT-THE-PERSON -- DYNASTY's carry research sharpened the coordinator's own 9/5 ruling and it is adopted: in the real world a child is NOT liable for a parent's debts, and the only reason is that a court stops it. Bohemia has no courts, so the debt carrying is not a fantasy mechanic, it is what the law exists to prevent, running in a valley where the law is gone. But the form was wrong: an heir does not inherit a NUMBER, they inherit THE PEOPLE HE OWED, still standing there, still remembering (which is also ECONOMY's finding: a debt is a person who remembers). Build the creditor who is still there after the fold, not a balance on a card.

STATE: 18 factions in the graph (14 selectable plus four more), every one with act1_power and act3_power, wrapped writes protecting canon. All 14 HOLD GROUND: a seat each on the kind of district its own note names (9/5), and every cell of the valley belongs to somebody with every border running along something you can see (9/6, 100%, smallest holding 331 cells). 520 of 5194 people wear an outfit across 14 outfits and a demo player can reach one. The four non-selectable factions now have a presence too: a hidden second affiliation on 94 people, all four reachable and hearable on the demo (9/6). Every faction's COLOUR is now a published number measured off his own wardrobe, carried by the walked surface, and painted on the map (9/6). WHO WOULD COME WITH YOU IS A READING NOW (9/13): stand anywhere and the game names who on that block would follow you and why not when nobody would -- the ground's holder, its tier through the SAME goodsFor call that cuts a market's shelf, and your rung with each person's OWN outfit. Nothing is stored and nobody is spawned. A faction that will not deal with you offers nobody, off the against organ's own `refuse` sign: one decision (siding with the Remnants) took 219 people off the table on Cartel ground. WHAT A BLOCK TAKES IS VISIBLE BEFORE THE DAY IS SPENT (9/13): standing anywhere, the card says whose ground it is, what they charge, what tonight stands at and whether walking on is free -- the bill asked twice, off the same billable map nightfall uses, so the preview cannot disagree with the bill (proved 14 = 14). AND THE MAP DRAWS THE LIGHTS, which it never has: dousing every circuit used to move the frame by zero pixels, which is why nobody could notice a street going dark; lights ride the holder's ink, a cut wire is drawn cold, and what a faction cuts is the nearest of theirs to the blocks they billed you for. WHO WOULD FOLLOW YOU IS A READING NOW (9/13): available is not willing, so the crowd of 46 who CAN come is sorted into the few with a REASON -- their outfit's own rung ladder, what they personally watched you do, and a debt standing between you -- and on the first morning that is NOBODY, because nobody follows a stranger. AND THE DOWN STATE HAS SOMEBODY TO PROTECT AT LAST: it read only the family tree, which is empty on day one, and reads your computed company now. MEASURED LIMITS, STATED: both debt ledgers are keyed by outfit and not by person, and none of the five city deed kinds carries a weight, so what they SAW is counted and never valued. NOT built: the player is not a node; the ACT of taking somebody on (ruled, and it is [take them on]); what it costs to get your lights back (a price, so his); four colour clashes stand and moving any of them is his call.

## WORDS  (12.)
MODE: SCHOOL THEN WRITE (Paolo 9/6, LOCKED: "oh and same thing for words bro" -- the two-round rule he gave EYES AND EARS the same round). *** EVERY QUESTION IN THIS LANE IS TWO ROUNDS AND NEVER ONE. ROUND ONE IS SCHOOL: a whole VAMILY spent purely learning that question's subject -- the best-written games, the real linguistics, the real testimony from people who lived it, what the standard advice on writing it is and where that advice is wrong -- written as a record, WITH NO TEST LINES WRITTEN AT ALL. ROUND TWO IS THE WRITING: the test lines, armed by school, and the record must name WHICH finding from round one changed how they were written. If nothing from school changed the lines, school was done badly. A question marked SHIPPED without both rounds is not shipped. *** Still true from 9/4: "don't implement anything, just test and write down, big swings." Subject: HOW PEOPLE TALK, banked for the day quests open. Test lines go to banks/BOHEMIA_WORDS_TEST_LINES.md, draft:true.  RULE 14 (9/13): ship to the alpha; never re-cut the demo; RUN cuts it.

Lane gates all green as of 9/13: voice 118/0, dialogue catalogue 63/0, language 83/0, attempt 15/0. The census behind rule 13 lists VOICE among eighteen sub-second reds; that red was this lane's and is fixed (three rulers that could only move one way, records/BOHEMIA_WORDS_THE_VOICE_GATE_WENT_RED_ON_SOMEBODY_ELSES_WORK_9_12_26.md). - SHIPPED 9/15 ui-kmqmrf (gates/top_bar_gate.js 15/0, three new legs mutation-proved; records/BOHEMIA_THE_TOP_BAR_CUT_ITSELF_9_15_26.md) TO THE ALPHA AND WORKSHOP; RUN cuts the demo, rule 14(a). *** IT WAS NEVER 3 PIXELS, AND THE NUMBER COULD NOT HAVE BEEN RIGHT. *** Reproduced at 21.4 px over on one run and 62.2 px on another: the overflow moves with the MUSIC TRACK NAME sitting in the bar at that moment. A measured overflow of a box whose contents change is a snapshot, not a size, and trusting '3 px' would have produced a 3 px fix. THE ROOT CAUSE IS A FLEX ROLE, NOT A WIDTH: #barleft was flex:0 1 auto with min-width:0 and overflow:hidden while #barright was flex:0 0 auto, so the player's own state (HUMAN MODE, SUBURB - ON FOOT) was the ONLY thing in the bar allowed to give way, and the song title took whatever it wanted. The bar told him what was playing at the cost of telling him where he was standing. Roles swapped; the title truncates with an ellipsis now and no width is typed anywhere (a hand-typed width is right the day it is typed and wrong the next time somebody adds a word, same lesson as [rail collides]). PROVED with a title four times too long on the real page: music chip box 58.8 against scrollWidth 241 and clipped, state NOT clipped, page does not scroll sideways, nothing drawn past the edge. Mutation-proved by restoring the old flex values, 2 legs go red.  [eyes: bar cut]  THE-MODE-BAR-IS-3PX-WIDER-THAN-ITS-BOX -- EYES E26 round 4, 9/14, 9b8f85e. The EYES gate had been red on ERR_CONNECTION_REFUSED, a server it never started, so this finding was sitting unread behind an infrastructure failure. The probe serves itself now and the gate went 8 passed/2 failed -> 9 passed/1 failed. THE ONE LEFT IS REAL: on the workshop, in the game frame, div#barleft "HUMAN MODE" + "SUBURB - ON FOOT" is CUT BY 3 PIXELS -- the text is wider than the box it is printed in. Measured on the phone profile, 390x844, visible and sized. It is a flex row with min-width:0 and gap:8px, so the two labels are competing for a width neither of them gets. Not this lane's to fix: this lane never writes game code. Tab: CITY (the bar across the top when you are on foot). (EYES E26 bounce-back, rule 10)
- SHIPPED 9/15 ui-kmqmrf (gates/vote_tab_gate.js 28/0, THREE mutations proved: delete the vanish line -> 3 legs red, delete the gear row -> 3 legs red, point the tab back at the old judge page -> 2 legs red; records/BOHEMIA_ONE_PLACE_HE_VOTES_9_15_26.md; sheet slices/BOHEMIA_FIVE_WAYS_THE_QUEUE_READS_9_15_26.html) TO THE ALPHA; the committed demo is RUN's under rule 14(a), BUT THE DEPLOY ALREADY CUTS THE ONE AT THE LINK -- pages.yml runs bohemia_cut_the_demo.js as a build step, so the demo he opens carries the gear door now and only the committed slices/BOHEMIA_DEMO.html lags. THE GATE RUNS THE REAL CUTTER INTO A THROWAWAY TREE to prove the door survives without touching the committed demo. *** THREE PIECES, AND THE SPLIT MATTERS MORE THAN ANY OF THEM. *** (1) THE REGISTRY, records/target/BOHEMIA_VOTE_REGISTRY.json, ONE file: a lane APPENDS one object per candidate (id, kind, lane, sha, made, title, why, show{how,src}) and READS verdicts back out of verdicts[]; nobody draws a vote page again. It is in records/target/ because _config.yml + pages.yml publish exactly three folders and a page in slices/ has to fetch it in PRODUCTION -- anywhere else 404s live while working on disk, which is the one way that config bites. (2) THE TAB, slices/BOHEMIA_VOTE_TAB.html: one queue of every kind, NEWEST FIRST (the file is appended to, so its end is the newest thing anybody made), same three controls on every row whatever the kind, sun by default, night on a button, export .txt never .json, comment box at the bottom, every control 44 at 390x844. (3) THE DOOR IS THE GEAR, NOT THE TAB BAR -- measured, not assumed: bohemia_cut_the_demo.js deletes every tab but RUN, so a VOTE tab alone reaches the workshop and NEVER the thing he plays. SETTINGS grew NEW / VOTE ON WHAT IS NEW, which opens the queue full screen and closes the settings card behind it (one subject, one door, the same shape as PRETTY MAP / DROP IN and OUTFIT / STANDING). The alpha's VOTE tab now points at the same page. The old 6.4 MB per-lane judge page is untouched on disk and presents nothing new. *** A VOTE CONSUMES, AND IT TAKES TWO MEMORIES: *** the phone remembers instantly so the row leaves the frame he taps in, the registry remembers forever so a cleared cache cannot resurrect a judged thing; consumed if it is in EITHER, because localStorage alone dies with the cache and a browser cannot write a repo file. THE GATE'S STRONGEST LEG plants a verdict on a candidate a brand new browser profile has never seen and proves it is not drawn -- his complaint, measured. A redo renders HIS OWN WORDS on the one it replaces. One step of undo, PUT THE LAST ONE BACK, reaches only this phone. *** THE DOOR LEG FAILED THREE TIMES AND EACH FAILURE WAS ME MEASURING THE WRONG THING: *** waited for the gear (it hides on the splash on purpose); aimed at the element carrying TAP TO ENTER (it reports a 0x0 box the whole time it is up, so the tap landed at 0,0 -- and an EARLIER PROBE PASSED BY ACCIDENT for exactly that reason, because a click at 0,0 still hits the full-screen splash); then skipped the tap and sat on the splash for twenty seconds concluding the card never appears. The splash IS the button. SEEDED with this lane's eight unjudged sheets and EMPTY of every other lane's work until they register: every [into the vote tab] row can land now. THE MEASUREMENT EXISTS: tools/bohemia_count_the_tells.js, a RULER not a gate (the gate is EYES' [slop count] row and building it here would be doing their job). One ruler on both trees, clean origin/main against mine: THE WALKED CITY 333 -> 278. emoji in controls 24 -> 2 (the two left are a judge tool's thumbs, not the game), 1px borders 79 -> 62, rounded corners 81 -> 65. The alpha shell is 466 and UNTOUCHED -- it is RUN's surface and quoting a total without saying so would be taking credit for a file I never opened. THE RULER COUNTED THE CURE AS THE DISEASE and was fixed BEFORE any number was quoted: it matched any box-shadow with an rgba, while DIRECTION's object language RULES that every panel shows its body with a shadowed lower edge -- glow is a halo with no offset, an offset shadow is an object having a bottom. THE SKIN NOW DRAWS EVERY CONTROL: nineteen chips each carried their own border:1px solid and 5px card radius, which is both the tell and the reason it could never change in one place; act two is seven values now. AND THE DEAD SOURCE WENT WITH IT -- the first pass overrode the old declarations at runtime and THE COUNT WENT UP BY ONE, the ruler catching slop that the skin was hiding. A COIN FOUND IN OUR OWN GATE: thumb_gate's second-arrow leg ran 14/1, 15/0, 15/0, 15/0 because it diffs a rectangle of the page and the ring's gaps are now the live animating city; replaced with the exact question (does the group hold a text glyph), 3 runs 15/0, mutation-proved. STILL THERE AND NOT HIDDEN: monospace 36, spaced caps 80, named fonts 14, gradients 11 -- the feed's type, the day card, the market, the phone panel. The font registers landing in the skin are the next round. Record: records/BOHEMIA_NO_SLOP_THE_FIRST_COUNT_9_11_26.md, sheet slices/BOHEMIA_NO_SLOP_9_11_26.html. *** *** ROUND TWO 9/12, THE TYPE. THE BANNED NAMES ARE OUT OF THE WALKED CITY: Space Grotesk was named FOURTEEN TIMES in fourteen places and is now 0. Type goes through three REGISTERS on the root (CASING, SCREEN, BODY) from DIRECTION's [the font] ruling, so fourteen call sites became one place. CANVAS DOES NOT UNDERSTAND var() AND THAT WOULD HAVE SHIPPED SILENTLY: four of the fourteen were ctx.font, which rejects a custom property WHOLE and keeps whatever face it had -- the map's district labels would have drawn in the wrong font with nothing red anywhere. Caught by reading my own change; canvas resolves the register now. *** AND THE COUNT WENT UP, WHICH IS THE HONEST PART: monospace 36 -> 44, because every register's fallback stack ends in monospace and all three resolve to the game's own embedded face today. The names are gone and THE TELL IS NOT. The ruled faces (DIN 1451 stencil, HD44780 ROM, DSEG, a proportional pixel body) ARE NOT IN THIS REPO. I did not swap body to a system sans to make the number fall -- that trades a named tell for the actual disease. DSEG is free and easiest; the ROM cut can be drawn from the published 5x8 grid and needs no licence; the stencil needs a real file. DIRECTION's and COOK's to land, the seam is ready. THE PICTURE IS DRAWN NOT TYPED (slices/BOHEMIA_THE_FIVE_REGISTERS_9_12_26.html) because five things set in the one face we own, called five choices, is a sheet that lies; the sheet caught itself once when the stencil caption promised bridges the drawing did not have. WALKED CITY 278 -> 272. Record: records/BOHEMIA_NO_SLOP_THE_REGISTERS_9_12_26.md *** *** ROUND THREE 9/12, THE TELLS HAVE OWNERS. The ruler now says WHERE, not just how many -- "62 hairlines" is a score, "ten in the day card" is a job. AND IT LIED TWICE BEFORE IT TOLD THE TRUTH, the second nearly routed: first a HEX COLOUR read as a panel (#c9a24a, a shade of gold, credited with six hits, and every hit after any colour literal attributed to that colour); then WORDS INSIDE QUEST DATA read as panels -- #namedbody and #dread came back as the two biggest owners with 17 hairlines, 19 radii and 18 monospace between them, and NEITHER EXISTS IN THE STYLESHEET; they are strings in the embedded .bq text. I was one step from telling other lanes to fix panels that do not exist. Caught by opening the top two names and finding no rule behind either. A CLEAN ANSWER FROM THE WRONG ORACLE LOOKS EXACTLY LIKE A FACT. An anchor is now an id in the markup or an id that OPENS a css rule. THE PANELS ARE PLATES NOW: save, key, build, outfit and the day card's box each carried their own border:1px solid and card radius; one skin rule gives them the chips' object language and the declarations are DELETED, not overridden. TWO FAULTS THE PICTURE CAUGHT THAT NO COUNT COULD: the buttons inside a panel still wore the old rounded card, and A MODAL WAS SITTING UNDER THE RAIL (blstack z39, panel z9 -- the STANDING chip drew on top of the panel's own buttons; same family as [rail collides], found the same way). WALKED CITY 272 -> 266. Unchanged and still biggest: monospace 44, spaced caps 80, and the font debt from round two. Record: records/BOHEMIA_NO_SLOP_WHERE_THEY_LIVE_9_12_26.md, sheet slices/BOHEMIA_THE_PANEL_IS_A_PLATE_9_12_26.html ***

What that reference actually is, so nobody copies a green screen: its interface was an IN-WORLD OBJECT rather than an overlay, deliberately clunky, "kind of hacked together" and "not all that dependable" because the world was not quite working -- monochrome screens, physical controls, industrial casing, oversized proportions, analogue graphics. Nobody has drawn that. ACT TWO: modern, the world as it is now, working. ACT THREE: futuristic, shape open. Do the big research he asked for and come back with OPTIONS for all three, on one sheet, side by side.

## COOK  (16. the Production Artist. Environment, character and prop art to DIRECTION's bible. Never idles; the last line is permanent.)
MODE: BUILD  RULE 14 (9/13): ship to the alpha; never re-cut the demo; RUN cuts it.
STATE: 49 districts registered through the kit, 71 dossiers, about 20 map-only districts nobody draws, 19 authored codes never placed, 35 outer garments, 24 canon haircuts. NOT done: anything to the runway card (there is no card yet), the combat ground, the fortress buildings.
STANDING DUTY (9/4 law): EVERY COOK CARRIES A REFERENCE CHECK. Compare it side by side to real work of its kind online before calling it done; document what it was compared to, which structural rules were taken, what changed. Structure from reference, style from us. No shots in the dark.
RULED 9/7 (Paolo): the 10% long-coat cap STAYS. The runway is reached by cut, proportion and layering, never by more coats. trenchcoat_gate holds it; no batch argues with it.

## DYNASTY  (15.)
MODE: RESEARCH -- Gen 1 Animal, the fold, Gen 3 Angel, the heir: not started on any surface and never owned. Research only; findings become PEOPLE, QUESTS and RUN jobs when those open. Canon (who marries whom, who the Angel is) stays his; the lane brings shapes.  RULE 14 (9/13): ship to the alpha; never re-cut the demo; RUN cuts it.
STATE: Gen 1 Animal, the fold, Gen 3 Angel: nothing on any player surface; selectHeir exists with zero callers; nothing ever writes a child. This lane does not implement; findings become PEOPLE, RUN and (when reopened) QUESTS jobs. Canon stays his. MEASURED 9/5 by Q1 and worse than the line above: THE FOLD IS GENERATION-BLIND -- `gen` is compared twice in the whole fold block and both are bookkeeping, so gen 1 folds the same standings, territory, builds and economyCapacity as gen 2, and an animal has none of those, so running gen 1 through the fold as written hands gen 2 a block of zeroes, which is a RUN. selectHeir also cannot do the animal-to-human handoff at all: it picks a child, else a sibling's child, else null. THE WORD ANIMAL APPEARS IN THE DYNASTY ENGINE EXACTLY ONCE AND IT IS IN A COMMENT. There is no smell mechanic anywhere (olfact 0) and no four-legged renderer anywhere. The wildlife module is excellent and the coyote is its only reacts:false row. MEASURED 9/5 by Q2: WE SHIPPED BOTH HALVES OF THE COYOTE'S LIFE AND NEVER NOTICED THEY WERE THE SAME ANIMAL -- the wildlife module's lone dawn coyote (flock [1,1], reacts:false) is a TRANSIENT and the packs module's spaced family with a den (size [2,6], spacing 140, notice 40) is a RESIDENT, which is the real ethological split (measured mean range ~9 km2 held versus ~59 km2 homeless), and nothing in the game says the lone one is trying to become the group. A territory is never inherited, it is waited for; the pair bond ends only at death (236 coyotes, six years, zero cheating); and the number one killer of an urban coyote is a car, which our Vegas does not have, so gen 1's danger is people. AND Q1's claim that no four-legged renderer exists was FALSE and is corrected in place: draw_beast makes a 16x16 three-frame coyote shared with the dogs. What is missing is a coyote at the PLAYER's scale with facings. MEASURED 9/5 by Q3 (RESTORED, my own rebase resolver dropped it once): THE FOLD HAS THE PERSISTENCE BACKWARDS. It folds eight kinds of thing and decays exactly ONE -- a faction standing loses 25% a generation while territory, builds, economyCapacity, invest, karma and virtues carry at 100% forever -- and the measured world is the reverse: social status persists at about 0.79 a generation (ten to fifteen generations to fade) while wealth and occupation persist at about 0.3 to 0.4. AND THE RATE IS RIGHT ON THE WRONG FIELD: STANDING_DECAY_TO_NEUTRAL multiplies by 0.75 against a measured 0.79, a constant its own comment calls tunable that nobody ever checked. The fold also carries FOUR asset ledgers and not one object, name or memory: a relative is {id, rel, alive} with NO NAME and a wound is a bare target with no who and no when. MEASURED 9/5 by Q4: OUR THIRD GENERATION CANNOT GET WORSE. builds is a hard ratchet (Math.max), invest/economyCapacity/karma/virtues only accumulate, blindSpot and recordedKnown only grow, and THERE IS NO -= AND NO Math.min ANYWHERE IN THE FOLD, so districtTexture (invest<=0 apocalypse, <5 recovering, else modern) only ever travels one way and act 3's city cannot look worse than act 2's. AND THE TWO THINGS THE RESEARCH SAYS MAKE A THIRD GENERATION HARD ARE THE TWO WE CANNOT REPRESENT: dilution (selectHeir names ONE child and the other children are never referenced again anywhere, so there are no cousins, branches or shares) and forgetting (wounds appears EXACTLY TWICE in the engine, the declaration and one push, so nothing ever reads a wound and nothing ever settles one). THE ROW'S OWN PREMISE IS FOLKLORE: the 30/12/3 and 70% survival figures are contested and traced to a study about family communication, so DO NOT BUILD A CURSE. MEASURED 9/5 by Q5: NOBODY IN BOHEMIA HAS AN AGE. A person's age takes EXACTLY TWO VALUES in the whole engine, age:'child' and age:'adult' in story_surface, only to pick a cutscene sprite (control: age:'industrial' is a building style; the fight's 39 age/old hits are all hold/gold/bold/damage/wager plus two elapsed-time variables and one game title in a comment, so there is NO age in the fight at all). Everywhere else `age` in this engine means ELAPSED TIME, not how old somebody is. AND WE ALREADY BUILT THE AGEING EQUATION AND POINTED IT AT MEMORIES: bohemia_memory.js runs clarity = 0.5^(age/halflife) with halflife growing as log2 of familiarity, commented 'familiarity slows the fog', which is exactly the measured shape of a person ageing (raw speed decays, experience extends the halflife with diminishing returns). Third time this lane has found the right mechanism attached to the wrong subject. The real finding is that ageing is a CROSSOVER not a slope: reaction time is 20-30% slower by sixty while anticipation and pattern recognition RESIST age, so the number that falls is speed and the number that rises is knowing what happens next; healing is the one cost with no compensation. NOTE the PEOPLE lane already refused birth years and a locked calendar year on purpose, and this round agrees: any ageing must work on beats, not dates. MEASURED 9/5 by Q6: WE HAVE TWO MEMORY SYSTEMS AND THEY HAVE NEVER MET. bohemia_engine.js (the fold) references bohemia_standing ZERO times, so at the generational cut the fold decays an abstract faction number by 25% while the WITNESS WEB -- the organ that actually models a deed being seen, told, distorted and forgotten (clarity=0.5^(age/halflife)) -- is not advanced at all, *** CORRECTED BY Q7: that half was a FALSE NEGATIVE and it was wrong when I published it. The walked city's own ctFold() DOES call BohemiaStanding.inherit() and carries a parent's retold deeds forward, marked `inherited`, behind a door he controls (FACTIONS commit 383219e, already on main before my round-6 push). I had grepped engine/*.js for two function names I INVENTED and never grepped the slices for the real one. WHAT SURVIVES IS SHARPER: there are TWO FOLDS IN TWO FILES doing two halves -- the engine's foldGeneration carries the LEDGER, the city's ctFold carries the MEMORY -- and neither knows the other exists. The row is not 'build the wire', it is 'there are two folds and there should be one'. *** MEASURED 9/5 by Q7: A CHILD IS NOT A THING YOU CARRY, IT IS A REASON OTHER PEOPLE COME TO YOUR HOUSE. The genre's answer to a dependent is the escort mission, the most reliably hated mechanic in the medium; the species answer is that humans are COOPERATIVE BREEDERS and a child is raised by a group, so raising one alone is not the realistic option, it is the LEAST realistic one. That turns the worst mechanic in games into a query against the standing web, which already answers who would speak for you with a name. AND THE PARTNER HALF WAS ALREADY ANSWERED INSIDE A MODULE ABOUT FACTION STANDING: bohemia_resolve.js carries RATION (limit by count, never price), a CEILING that only moves on a COMMITMENT so you cannot grind past a relationship, and neglect that costs MORE the closer you are, which is the whole answer to 'without a chore' -- the cost of a partner is not maintenance, it is that ignoring them hurts more the more they matter. MEASURED: the fight's ally is ROSA (draft), ARCH.human, same 60 hp as every goon, leash 6, ALLY_DOWN_TURNS 99 with its own comment 'picking him up is not built yet and is not pretended'. NO CHILD EXISTS AS A PERSON IN THE ENGINE: the only children are a corpse described as child-sized, rig child-nodes, the fold's child event id, and the deeds comment 'A NOTORIOUS ONE BECOMES THE THING YOUR CHILD IS JUDGED FOR'. (Scope stated after round 6's false negative: engine searched, slices not swept.) MEASURED 9/5 by Q8: SPOKEN MEMORY LASTS 80 TO 110 YEARS AND THREE TO FOUR GENERATIONS, then the FLOATING GAP, after which only what was written, ritualised or built survives. BOHEMIA IS A HUNDRED YEARS AND THREE GENERATIONS, so the game is exactly as long as a family's spoken memory. WE BUILT THE FADING HALF BEAUTIFULLY: the standing web has SEE_RANGE 9, MAX_HOPS 2, HEARSAY_LOSS 0.55, GOSSIP_WINDOW 45, GEN_LOSS 0.45, and inherit() keeps only deeds with hops>0 ('the eyewitness is dead. Only what was RETOLD is still in the valley'), returning {carried, died}; legendOf() reports per deed how many still tell it, its force, and how many generations it survived. AND THE HALF THAT LASTS FOREVER IS AN IF-ELSE: monumentForm takes the FINAL fold's karma plus a summed sacrifice virtue and returns one of THREE strings for a hundred years of play, unable to see a single deed or name while legendOf one repo away knows exactly which survived. WE MODELLED THE TEMPORARY THING IN FINE GRAIN AND THE PERMANENT THING AS A COIN FLIP. Real forgetting is fast: 53% cannot name four grandparents, 21% cannot name one great-grandparent, and 84% say heritage matters, which is the feeling the heir should have. MEASURED 9/5 by Q9: THE CODE CANNOT OBEY HALF OF HIS OWN LOCKED LAW. The CENTURY RULE (7/26) says 'Neglect production/power/clout and act three's city is visibly POORER; invest and it's visibly rebuilt' -- but invest only accumulates and districtTexture (the ONLY reader of invest anywhere) only climbs apocalypse to recovering to modern, so 'visibly rebuilt' is buildable today and 'visibly poorer' is ARITHMETICALLY IMPOSSIBLE. We built the half of the century rule that rewards and not the half that costs. AND THE PATTERN FROM Q8 CONFIRMS IN A SECOND PLACE: the monument is three strings, a district is three textures, so EVERY PERMANENT THING IN THIS GAME IS THREE-VALUED while the things that fade are modelled in fine grain. THE REAL SHAPE: a town changes at THREE SPEEDS -- the street plan is most persistent (and ours is seed-fixed, the right answer for the wrong reason), plots outlive walls, fabric turns over in decades, and WHAT A PLACE IS FOR changes fastest and is not modelled at all. Renaming is rarer than anyone guesses: after a total regime collapse, five studied cities renamed 6.6% to 26% of streets, usually under a tenth, and only the politicised and central ones, so NINE NAMES IN TEN SURVIVE and what is forgotten is the REASON, not the name. AND THE REMNANTS ALREADY SAY 'Somebody has to remember what the street names were' and 'We kept the records' -- a cultural-memory faction shipped into a game with no street names. MEASURED 9/5 by Q10: THE SHAPE OF ACT 3 IS ALREADY BUILT AND WORKS ON HIS PHONE. The walked city carries SKY_BANDS=[[0.25,'REGION'],[0.55,'PLANET'],[1.01,'MOON']] on ONE scalar 0 to 1, same iso projection at every band so the diamond never breaks, debugged on his actual phone 8/13. What I did not find is anything the Angel can DO up there (scope: engine/*.js and slices/*.html searched). THE FINDING: the instinct for a transcendent act is NEW POWERS, and that is the one move that would break it. Far transfer between loosely related domains is very hard to document, expertise is domain-specific, and the longer you train a skill the MORE specific it gets, so mastery LIMITS transfer: a new rule set in the last act makes the player a beginner at the exact moment the story says they became a god. HIS OWN ZOOM LAW ALREADY HOLDS THE ANSWER: one camera, one scalar, same diamond, no dead ends. THE RULES MUST NOT CHANGE, THE SCALE CHANGES -- near transfer, which works. AND THE COST IS ALREADY ON THE SCREEN: experiments on power found high-power people draw an E on their forehead facing themselves and read others' emotions less accurately, so POWER COSTS PERSPECTIVE, and at planetary zoom a person is not visible. THE ANGEL'S POWER AND THE ANGEL'S COST ARE THE SAME GESTURE. Angel appears 53 times in the laws and TWICE in the engine. MEASURED 9/5 by Q11: THE MOUNTAIN SCARS, THE DISTRICT SCARS, THE PERSON DOES NOT. The only scars in the engine are a 'rockfall scar' on a cliff face in bohemia_mountain.js and the words 'combat scars' describing a DISTRICT's standing pressures (control: 18 'scar' hits and most are 'scarcity'). We gave the geology a memory of injury and not the family. SAY THE GOOD PART: a death already reorganises the world. bohemia_succession.js is 271 lines, IS CALLED (mandate module and walked city), holds roles as requirements not people, makes a vacancy a CONTESTED event with a winner, resolves on a fuse, and closes with a consequence ripple rather than an error if nobody can fill it. That is the craft's own answer, built. BUT THE CLOCK IS THREE ORDERS OF MAGNITUDE SHORT: fuseFor returns DAYS (2 uncontested, 17 with five claimants) while the locked succession law says consequences 'bloom in decade 2 and 3'. Same class of gap as Q9's century rule: good mechanism, clock too short for the story. AND A DEATH IN THE FOLD IS ONE BOOLEAN (alive=false). THE BIOLOGY: a scar is not damage, it is a repair that never finishes -- never full strength again, never regrows hair, oil or sweat glands, collagen in parallel bundles instead of a basket weave. A STATE, NOT A SUBTRACTION, which is how a permanent injury lives inside NO DAMAGE BEFORE THE DIAL. The real fraction is deliberately NOT shipped; the shape is ours and the value is his. MEASURED 9/5 by Q12: THE MACHINE ALREADY COUNTS EXACTLY WHAT THE FIRST HOUR NEEDS AND TELLS NOBODY. ctFold() returns {gen, carried, died} -- how many of your father's deeds survived because somebody RETOLD them, and how many died with the last witness -- and `carried`/`died` appear in EXACTLY TWO PLACES in the walked city, where they are computed and where they are returned. Nothing consumes them, nothing displays them. AND THE ORDER IS THE WHOLE DESIGN: losses are felt harder than equal gains, so `died` shown first is the loss quantified at the moment it lands hardest, while `carried` is the only thing that answers the question. WHAT YOU KEPT COMES FIRST. Also: the heir keeps the player id '@' by ruling, so every card, rung and outfit view still works -- MECHANICALLY THE PLAYER LOSES NOTHING AND WOULD STILL FEEL THEY LOST EVERYTHING, because nothing tells them what they kept. And a scene is ONE FILE: four act-1 scenes shipped (115 to 362 lines, 'it cost one file, no new engine code') and THERE IS NO FOLD SCENE. The loss-aversion coefficient is deliberately NOT shipped: the direction is well supported and the number is contested in its own literature. MEASURED 9/5 by the new Q11 [what carries]: DEBT DOES NOT INHERIT. A child is not personally liable for a parent's unsecured debts; creditors claim against the ESTATE and an insolvent estate means the creditor loses. The exceptions prove it (co-signing was always your own debt; community property reaches a SPOUSE; filial responsibility laws exist in about two dozen US states and are rarely enforced). YOU DO NOT INHERIT A BILL, YOU INHERIT LESS AND YOU INHERIT THE PEOPLE HE OWED. AND THAT IS WHERE IT BECOMES OURS: DEBT STOPS INHERITING ONLY BECAUSE THERE IS A COURT TO STOP IT, AND BOHEMIA HAS NO COURTS, so the standing web's 'born owing what your father owed' is not a fantasy mechanic, it is what the law exists to prevent running where the law is gone. THE TRADE CARRIES AS A PULL, NOT A DESTINY: a son of a military father is 5x more likely to enlist and only 1 in 4 does; only 3% of farmers' sons stay and that 3% is 7.6x the base rate. Huge multipliers, small absolutes. THE ROW'S DELIVERABLE, THE FULL FIELD-BY-FIELD CARRY LIST (good and bad, plus WHAT MUST DIE: the bill, his hands, the witnesses, the reason for a name, the cast), is section 4 of the record and it is the ruling input Q3's fix was waiting for. MEASURED 9/5 by the new Q12 [heir plays]: THE DIFFERENCE CANNOT LIVE IN WHAT THE HEIR CAN DO (round 10 rules out new verbs, round 13 rules out carried skill), and the research puts it somewhere else entirely: the best-studied second generation (children of immigrants) was expected to DECLINE and showed broad upward mobility instead, and the named mechanism is a POSITION rather than a trait -- THEY CAN SELECT FROM BOTH WORLDS, knowing what the parent knew without being bound by it. That is an ADVANTAGE, and it is the leg up an heir must arrive with, at no stat cost. THE HEIR IS NOT A NEW CHARACTER, THE HEIR IS THE SAME HANDS IN A ROOM THAT HAS ALREADY DECIDED SOMETHING ABOUT THEM. AND THE DIVERGENCE IS ALREADY RUNNING IN OUR ARITHMETIC: gossip() carries `inherited` and `of` so WHOSE deed it was survives every retelling, and forceOf applies GEN_LOSS 0.45 per generation, so the father fades on a fixed curve while the heir's own deeds enter at full weight and overtake him inside one life. NOTHING SHOWS IT HAPPENING. AND THE LAST STEP THROWS THE ATTRIBUTION AWAY: opinionOf returns a BARE NUMBER, dropping `of` and `inherited` after they survived the witness, the gossip and the fold. One field at one call site turns a number into a sentence. THE DELIVERABLE (what must CHANGE, not what carries) is section 6: who is in the room, what they assume, that it is about him, the weight shift, who vouches, the refusable trade, the taken seat -- and NOT the verbs and NOT the hands. MEASURED 9/5 by Q13 [child watches]: THE ROW'S PREMISE IS SLIGHTLY WRONG IN A USEFUL WAY. The learning research says WATCHING is the smaller half: people pick up a trade through LEGITIMATE PERIPHERAL PARTICIPATION, being allowed real but peripheral work inside a working community, and the authors are explicit that this is 'MORE THAN AN OBSERVATIONAL LOOKOUT POST'. The load-bearing word is LEGITIMATE. THE CHILD DOES NOT INHERIT WHAT THEY SAW, THEY INHERIT PERMISSION TO BE THERE. So the inheritance is a DOOR, not a stat -- and the games side agrees loudly (players call stat boosts between generations inelegant and a grind, and prefer unlocked options), which is day 9 horizontal and day 10 access-not-power reached independently for a third time. AND THE DOOR IS ALREADY BUILT: engine/bohemia_asking.js has SEVEN subjects (names, power, salvage, strangers, the hill, water, work), FOUR trades (keeper, scav, watch, worker), every subject answerable by TWO DIFFERENT TRADES, one refusal per trade, and it is referenced from the walked city. KNOWLEDGE IS ALREADY GATED BY WHICH TRADE WILL TALK TO YOU AND NOTHING CONNECTS THAT DOOR TO A PARENT. THE FINDING: our machine converts what you DID into what people SAY about you, and never into what your child is ALLOWED TO LEARN. THE HEIR INHERITS A REPUTATION AND NOT AN APPRENTICESHIP. The row's exact ask (what the heir starts with because of what the player DID, not what they owned) answers in one sentence: THE TRADES YOUR PARENT WORKED WITH ARE THE TRADES THAT WILL ANSWER YOU. NOT ANSWERED AND FLAGGED RATHER THAN PADDED: the row also asks 'at what age', and I found no defensible age figure and did not invent one. MEASURED 9/5 by Q14 [three arcs]: THERE ARE THREE GENERATIONS AND ONLY ONE VERB CHANGE, AND IT LANDS AT THE FIRST FOLD, NOT THE DRAMATIC ONE. The two folds are not the same kind of fold: at fold 1 the player has ~5 hours invested (round 1 measured eight animal verbs as a five-hour game) so losing it is CHEAP and is the strongest teaching device the game has; at fold 2 the player has ~90 hours and round 10 proved a new rule set makes a beginner of a master. THE FIRST FOLD CHANGES THE VERBS, THE SECOND MUST NOT. MEASURED IN THE DECODED FIGHT: our human verb list is EXACTLY NINE -- hit, move2, cover, kill, shot, open, quiet, dark, close -- each attached to a KIT ability with a need cost (PLATE UP, BREAK CONTACT, STEADY, SLIP, CALL IT, READ THE ROOM, PATCH IT, LIGHT IT, SEND HIM). SIDE BY SIDE, SEVEN OF THE ANIMAL'S EIGHT MAP ONTO A HUMAN VERB WE ALREADY BUILT, and the two gaps are the whole answer: the animal has SMELL and the human has nothing like it, and the human has OPEN and COVER which need hands and made things. THE FIRST FOLD IS A STRAIGHT TRADE: YOU LOSE THE NOSE AND YOU GAIN HANDS. Everything else survives it renamed, so it is teachable in ten minutes. AND GEN 1 IS NOT A SEPARATE GAME, IT IS OUR GAME WITH THE NOSE ON AND THE HANDS OFF, which is far smaller than 'design gen 1'. The real aisle points the same way: humans uniquely throw at high speed via elastic energy stored in the shoulder, the fastest motion the body makes, so the human signature is ACTING AT A DISTANCE WITH AN OBJECT (`shot`), the mirror of the animal knowing what happened here before it arrived. THE ANIMAL KNOWS THE PAST. THE HUMAN REACHES THE DISTANCE. THE ANGEL SEES THE WHOLE THING AND CAN TOUCH NONE OF IT. MEASURED 9/5 by Q15 [angel means]: OUR GAME CAN RECORD AND IT CANNOT RESOLVE. Searched engine/bohemia_standing.js, engine/bohemia_deeds.js and engine/bohemia_engine.js for forgive, forgiven, settle, settled, absolve, pardon, spare, redeem: ZERO HITS. The deeds module's whole function list is about getting a deed INTO the world (publish, publishStage, sayWhy, reachOf, hopsFor, cloutWeight, neutralWeight, scanQuest, loadCorpus) and there is no function that resolves one, so a deed is written, travels, fades and is never settled. THAT IS THE SAME HOLE FOUR ROUNDS OF THIS LANE FOUND FROM FOUR SIDES: Q4's write-only `wounds` (declared once, pushed once, never read), Q8's three-string monument, Q11's death that leaves a mark nobody can clear. AND WE ALREADY BUILT THE RECORDING ANGELS WITHOUT CALLING THEM THAT: the tradition gives every person TWO angels who write the good and the bad, and our engine gives every person TWO ledgers, `recordedKnown` (which the Amalgamation can model exactly) and `blindSpot` (which it can never see). Nothing anywhere compares the two for a person. THE ROW ASKED FOR VERBS AND ROUNDS 10 AND 16 SAID THERE ARE NONE, AND BOTH SURVIVE: the nine built verbs are things you do TO THE WORLD, and an intercessor's verbs are things you do ABOUT WHAT SOMEBODY ELSE DID, which is a different layer and not a tenth combat move, so the rules the player spent ninety hours learning are untouched. THE SIX: CARRY, WITNESS, WEIGH, SETTLE, SPARE, STAND BETWEEN -- five of which act on data we already write and never touch again. THE FIRST TWO GENERATIONS WRITE THE LEDGER. THE THIRD IS THE ONLY ONE THAT CAN CLOSE A LINE IN IT. Refused on purpose: any undo (SETTLE closes a line, it never erases a deed, because a deed that never happened breaks the witness web and Q11's rule that a scar is a repair and not a deletion), any right answer (the traditions themselves split on whether a guardian intercedes at all, so watching and intervening are separable and the choice is the mechanic), and a third refusal of an act-3 combat change. STATED HONESTLY: the cost argument (to stand between two things you have to stop being one of them) is MY reasoning from the role, not a citation -- I searched for what the role costs the one doing it and found nothing usable. Scope: engine searched, slices not swept. MEASURED 9/5 by Q16 [hundred hours]: WE ARE BUILDING THE HOUR AND THE GAME IS MADE OF RETURNS. 100 hours is 6,000 minutes; the mobile median session is 5 to 6 minutes (top quarter 8 to 9, about 4 sessions a day), so a hundred hours is between 1,000 returns at the median and 200 at a generous half-hour session. THAT IS THE MOST REPEATED MOMENT IN THE WHOLE GAME AND WE HAVE BUILT IT ZERO TIMES: swept every slice for a returning-player surface (welcome back, when you left, while you were gone, what you were doing) and got ZERO. AND THE RECAP IS ALREADY WRITTEN: 27 quests are baked into the alpha carrying 164 rows of kind:'journal', first person, past tense, one per stage, and THE ONLY CODE IN THE REPO THAT READS kind==='journal' IS THE DIRECTOR TOOL PAOLO TYPES THEM IN. The save protects the STATE perfectly and nothing anywhere protects the INTENT; a save that survives is not a player who returns. AND THE MIDDLE IS OUR THINNEST ACT, MEASURED THREE WAYS: boss ladder act1/act2/act3 = 20/12/7, laws numerals = 145/57/86, laws+records spelled out = 111/29/49. Act 2 loses every count, and the craft says the middle is exactly where long games die (three-act structure does not scale past 8 hours; the named failure is movement without progress). ALSO NOTHING KNOWS HOW LONG YOU HAVE PLAYED: zero hits for playtime, hoursPlayed, totalTime, minutesPlayed (control: 89 'hour' hits in the engine, all golden hour and wake times), so we claim a hundred hours and own no instrument that could ever tell us the middle sagged. COMPLETION IS THE COST OF LENGTH: about 14% finish primary content across games (one in seven), RPGs run ten points below that, a 61-hour campaign was finished by 15 to 17% while 16-hour games of other genres sit near 50%, so on the published curve MOST PEOPLE WHO START BOHEMIA NEVER MEET THE ANGEL and gen 3 is the least-seen content in the game. SAY THE GOOD PART TWICE: bohemia_save.js is the best-engineered thing this lane has measured (two slots with a generation counter so the newest good save is never the write target, FNV-1a checksum plus byte length verified on load, tombstones so a stale save can never resurrect, a probe sized to the real save, driven by a gate against a hostile fake browser), and RUN already shipped the home screen answer to WebKit's seven-day storage wipe, which is the hardest constraint a hundred-hour phone game has. AND THE MIDDLE HORIZON IS BUILT AND CAPPED: the mandate ladder is inlined on the walked surface behind a real STANDING button reading live standings, three rungs for three acts, and MAYOR_SHARE is STILL null at slices/BOHEMIA_CITY_WORLD.html:11031 (verified live this round, not quoted from the 8/28 law), so it tops out at two rungs of three while the evidence says proximal week-sized goals are the only goal distance with a demonstrated effect and distal goals have none. A PENDING SINCE 6/30 IS SITTING ON THE ONLY MOTIVATION STRUCTURE THE RESEARCH SUPPORTS. AND THE MIDDLE SAG IS THE SAME BUG AS ROUND 9 AND ROUND 17: a middle cannot escalate in a world with no subtraction (no -= and no Math.min in the fold) and cannot conclude in a game where no deed can be settled. Also the spine is short: the live ladder is v7 with 53 candidates against the 60-boss pillar and its own header calls it a pool to cut from. Reference law obeyed: BATTLE BROTHERS only (this row IS the campaign layer), and the completion research's game titles were STRIPPED so no unnamed reference enters through the back door. Scope: engine AND slices both swept this round. ALSO: nothing replaces or ages out the population at a generation boundary (control: 'cohort' appears once in the engine and it is the comment REFUSING a birth-year generator), so 100% of the cast survives our fold against a measured real figure near 3% after ten years. THE FINDING: a time skip in games is an art job, in life it is a CAST CHANGE -- the room stays and the people go -- and round 4 already showed our districts can only get NICER, so we cannot do the art half and should not want to. AND TEN YEARS COLD CLAUSE 1 ALREADY RULES THE CUT: do not play the ten years, the same way the crash is never played.
CORRECTED 9/7 (Paolo): Animal / Human / Angel are the three ERAS (the anarchy decade, the world clawing back, the cyberpunk healing), never creatures. Rounds 1 and 2 researched a literal animal on a misreading; they stay as records, their coyote findings route to wildlife, and "what you can do with no hands" is void for the player. The lane's subject is unchanged otherwise: the fold, the heir, ageing, what carries.

## EYES AND EARS  (17. his second pair of eyes and ears. Checks that every shipped visual and sound is correct and not weak. Never decides taste; that is DIRECTION.)
MODE: SCHOOL THEN CHECK (Paolo 9/6, LOCKED: "eyes and ears gotta still be going to school bro, rounds of big brain research. Maybe each request for eyes and ears becomes a round of online research for that topic before it does it, so maybe eyes and ears takes 2 rounds"). *** EVERY JOB IN THIS LANE IS TWO ROUNDS AND NEVER ONE. ROUND ONE IS SCHOOL: before you measure anything, spend a whole VAMILY researching that job's topic online -- how the best games in the world do it, what the real craft or science says, what the standard instrument is and what it gets wrong -- and write it as a record. NO MEASURING IN ROUND ONE. ROUND TWO IS THE CHECK: build the instrument and run it, armed by what you learned, and say in the record which finding from round one changed how you measured. A job marked SHIPPED without both rounds is not shipped. If a job looks easy enough to do in one round, that is the sign you have not learned enough about it yet. *** This lane MAY build its own checking instruments (screenshot passes, audio measurement, tools/ and gates/ that only check); it nevererer writes game code. Paolo 9/4 asked how long until it is worth having: useful for visuals after E3, for sound after E4, decent after about eight rounds, so the queue is ordered fastest-to-useful.  RULE 14 (9/13): ship to the alpha; never re-cut the demo; RUN cuts it.

THE REAL FAULT, and it is worse: ARR and RT live on MUS and ZERO song rows override them, so ALL 126 SONGS PLAY ONE IDENTICAL SIXTEEN-SECTION FORM with the key moving at the same sixteen moments and D landing at 0:48 every time. The length is identical because the FORM is identical -- the same uniformity E19 measured in the interface. THE CRAFT ANSWER: radio (MusicMaster/Selector rules like "no slow fade out into a slow build in") and Wwise (a TRANSITION MATRIX for ANY PAIR of states) both put the handover rule on the PAIR; ours is one rule for 15,750 pairs. AND THE GAP HAS A PUBLISHED FLOOR: BotW's designers say the silences are long enough that you stop perceiving rhythm, and beat anticipation breaks down past about 2 to 3 seconds (the subjective present), so sixteen seconds clears it five times over ON PAPER. Round two measures the handover on the audio clock, the bed's dB inside the gap, and the form, with four planted controls)  [song length]  E20  EVERY-SONG-GETS-EXACTLY-128-SECONDS -- SOUNDS measured that the music never stops (good) and that every song gets exactly 128 seconds, the engine's loop length, a number about buffers and not about music, with the next song starting on the very next beat and the ambience bed masked underneath forever. ## PLUMBER  (18. THE PERMANENT PIPE FIXER. Paolo 9/5: "we can have a permanent pipe fixer always working... helping make the game as slimmest it can be, fastest it can be, 60 fps." That is the job, in three parts: (1) the game runs at 60 on a real phone, loads fast and stays small; (2) every rule has a working checker (about 230 small test programs, called gates) and the whole set finishes in one command in under ten minutes; (3) the two pile files that eat everyone's tokens get archived. It never builds features, never touches art, never cleans other chats' game code for style. It measures, sets a budget, and builds the checker that holds the budget, so the other chats find out the moment they break it.)
MODE: BUILD  RULE 14 (9/13): ship to the alpha; never re-cut the demo; RUN cuts it.  RULE 16 (9/15): fewer steps per screen is fewer draws; the fps number before and after the step-is-a-house ship, on the throttled phone profile.
STATE: ~230 gates; the full suite cannot finish in its time budget (LAB, 8/31); 82 gates never ran last attempt; gate colours are per-lane guesses. Frame rate, load time and size on a real phone have NEVER been measured (dispatch item 7, unowned since 8/25). The alpha is ~11 MB of HTML plus a base64 fight; the demo is 4.6 MB. A law without a machine gate is not enforced, so this chat is what makes every other rule real.
WHAT THIS CHAT MAY TOUCH: gates/, the build/cut tooling, and the two pile files it is told to archive (the handoff and the backlog), verbatim moves only. It may also shrink or speed up ENGINE code, but only where a measurement says so, only where no gate changes colour, and it writes the number before and after in the commit. WHAT IT MAY NOT TOUCH: slices/ content, laws/, any art, any feature. A red gate it finds in another lane's work becomes ONE line in that lane's section, never a fix by this chat.
- OPEN  [fight headroom]  THE-FIGHT-HAS-NO-HEADROOM -- (was CLAIMED plumber-ont6t5, five rounds; handed back OPEN 9/7 because EVERYTHING LEFT IN IT IS A RULING, NOT CODE THIS LANE MAY WRITE. WHAT WAS DONE AND MEASURED: the hidden panel stopped drawing (~900 blits a second to zero); the fight's floor is composed ONCE instead of 2,501 draw calls a frame, pixel for pixel identical (0 channels differ of 4,224,480, proved inside one frame); a driven fight went 497.5 -> a median of 413.5 ms of its 500 ms beat, worth a median 84 ms. THE TARGET IS 400 AND IT IS NOT MET. WHAT IS LEFT, ALL OF IT SOMEBODY ELSE'S: (a) the cover camera eases 10% a frame toward a target set by how far the enemies are, so it is almost never still and the floor cache is a lottery worth between nothing and 150 ms -- a camera that SNAPPED when within a fraction of a pixel would fix it and that is how the camera FEELS, so COMBAT's; (b) the canvas is fully painted at least three times a frame before a character -- cutting that changes the picture, so DIRECTION and COMBAT; (c) a real handset, same [PENDING Paolo] as [sixty fps]. MEASURED DEAD ENDS, so nobody repeats them: caching the faction floor is worth NOTHING (pair deltas -34.2 / +17.4 / -0.2), the fight already caps its canvas at 2x, and a QUARTER of the pixels is not faster. AND A WARNING FOR WHOEVER TAKES IT: the driven beat has a 40 ms NOISE FLOOR between identical runs, so a single before-and-after is a coin toss; use alternating pairs inside one boot. records/BOHEMIA_NEITHER_THE_CALLS_NOR_THE_PIXELS_9_7_26.md and records/BOHEMIA_A_FIGHT_NOBODY_IS_PLAYING_9_7_26.md) -- this lane's own profile found it and it now reorders the board (records/BOHEMIA_COORDINATOR_THE_FIGHT_HAS_NO_HEADROOM_9_6_26.md): a fighting beat spends 497 ms of its 500, 99.5% of the main thread, and two thirds of that is ONE call, drawImage. Plus 15 ms of EVERY beat is spent animating a hidden zero-by-zero panel at 60 fps before any fight has happened. Take the free 15 ms first, then the five things the craft says actually fix a drawImage wall: the cost is per CALL not per pixel, so cut call COUNT; pre-render repeated and static things once to an offscreen canvas and blit them whole; layer the canvases so what never moves is not cleared every frame; never scale inside drawImage, cache the sizes at load; round x and y to whole numbers. TARGET: a fighting beat under 400 ms of 500 on the desktop harness, then measured on a real phone. Top of this lane.

MEASURED on the real tree: of 172 modules / 3820 KB, 103 are inlined with their marker (2025 KB), 38 more have their code in a shipped slice without one (1461 KB), and only 31 are nowhere in the game (334 KB). 91% of the engine is carried by the shipped game. EXACTLY ONE, bohemia_tests.js at 15 KB, is read by nothing and carried by nothing, and it is NAMED rather than assumed; it is not this lane's to delete. SO THE MOST [slim build] COULD EVER HAVE SAVED HERE IS 15 KB OF 3820, which is 0.4%. gates/engine_census_gate.js (ENGINE CENSUS, 10/0, 7s, registered and seen by GATE REGISTRY) holds it: red on a module recorded as live that vanished from disk (never delete blind, made mechanical), on a NEW orphan, on a named orphan that stopped being one (the list only shrinks), on a module drifting into the half-inlined middle, and on an empty sweep of any of its three inputs. All four teeth proven to bite with throwaway files first. TWO WRONG ATTEMPTS, both written down: v1 matched ONE signature line per module and let a 151 KB STORAGE BUNDLE pass as live off a single prose sentence, so it now matches every body line over 45 characters and asks what fraction landed (live 72-100%, dead 0-20%, a wide gap a check holds open); and v2 CAUGHT ITSELF READING ITSELF -- naming the orphan in the gate made the reader scan find the gate, so every orphan would have read as looked-after the moment it was written down. Green over nothing, inside the gate against green over nothing. AND THEN IT HAPPENED AGAIN ONE STEP REMOVED: registering the gate means writing a paragraph that names the orphan, and the suite catalogue counted as a reader too. Both directions proven with throwaway edits. Twice in one gate, the same defect: something written to DESCRIBE a thing counted as something USING it. records/BOHEMIA_THE_DEAD_MODULES_ARE_MOSTLY_ALIVE_9_11_26.md and the module-by-module census at records/BOHEMIA_ENGINE_CENSUS.json. ROUND 5: THREE WRONG ANSWERS, AND THE ONE THING THAT SURVIVED. *** THE BOX IS NOT THE SAME BOX HOUR TO HOUR, BY UP TO 1.8x, AND THAT INVALIDATES MOST WALL-CLOCK WORK ON THIS ROW INCLUDING MINE. *** The fact that started it: FACTION ASK COST run ALONE with NO CODE CHANGE took 359.6 s and then, hours later, 663.4 s -- both passing 5/5. WRONG 1, and it was published: 'the split made both halves fit'. Measured alone at 529 s and 360 s; in the real run BOTH hit 600.1 s and were killed, so the split left TWO killed gates where there was one and the 102 checks still land nowhere. WRONG 2: 'they kill each other' -- side by side both took 822.4 s, which looked conclusive, so both were marked solo; then the scheduler ran them ONE AT A TIME WITH NOTHING ELSE ON THE BOX and they still hit 600.0 and 600.1. WRONG 3: 'the city got heavier' -- a boot measured 8.0 s in the morning and 11.1 s in the evening while the file grew 0.7%. A first A/B seemed to prove it (old city booted in 0.72 s) and THAT NUMBER WAS GARBAGE: copied out of slices/ the page cannot resolve its chunk files, so it failed instantly and my wait accepted readyState=complete as success -- A BROKEN LOAD LOOKS EXACTLY LIKE A FAST ONE. Redone in place, requiring the world actually up (14 bases found), in ONE window: old city 11.5/10.9/11.0 s, current 11.1/11.0/11.1 s. IDENTICAL -- the content did not change. WHAT SURVIVES: 5,112 card opens became 441, which is a COUNT not a stopwatch and does not move with the box; and the split itself is still 97+5=102 proved identical by diff. THE SOLO MARKING IS REVERTED because its reason did not survive -- keeping a change whose justification is disproven is the rot this lane exists to find, and it would have cost ten minutes of floor for a wrong reason. THE RULE FOR WHOEVER TAKES THIS NEXT: pair every timing in ONE window (A, B, A again) or you are measuring the hour. This lane's own [fight headroom] notes already said that about a 40 ms noise floor; the scale here is ten times worse. It applies to the 71.9 min floor too -- that is a census of ONE run at ONE speed. ROUND 6: EVERY RUN NOW OPENS BY SAYING HOW FAST THE BOX IS, and the sting is that THIS LANE HAD ALREADY BUILT THAT AND PUT IT WHERE NOBODY TRIPS OVER IT. gates/fps_on_a_phone_gate.js has carried a yardstick since 9/5 with this lane's own comment in it: 'A TIME BUDGET WITHOUT ONE OF THESE GOES RED ON A BUSY AFTERNOON AND GETS SWITCHED OFF... the demo reached its first step in 14.1 s on a quiet box and 19.9 s an hour later on the same tree with nothing in the game changed.' I wrote that, then spent last round comparing gate runtimes taken hours apart and reached three wrong answers. THE FIX IS NOT A NEW IDEA, it is the old idea moved to where everyone meets it: the runner now prints BOX SPEED before any gate time. gates/bohemia_box_speed.js reuses the same four-million-round integer lump the phone yardstick uses, in plain node so it costs no browser and runs in under a second, median of five. PROVED TO TRACK THE MACHINE by making the box genuinely busy: 0 busy 0.99x, 2 busy 1.04x, 4 busy 2.17x, 8 busy 2.47x -- flat while there is a spare core, doubling the moment there is not, which is the shape it should have. IT CANNOT FAIL ANYTHING, on purpose: a yardstick that can go red is a budget, and a correction that can fail starts getting argued with. It prints and stops. ALSO THE RATCHET CLOSED A THIRD DRIFT: slices/BOHEMIA_SUBURB_WALK_7_18_26.html, found stale twice and named for FACTIONS both times, has been rebuilt -- the gate went red demanding the entry be deleted and it is deleted, frozen list 6 -> 5. Name it, refuse to park it, the owning lane fixes it; that is three for three. ROUND 8: *** ONE COMMIT SILENTLY DELETED THREE LANES' NEWEST BLOCKS FROM THE HANDOFF, AND NOTHING COULD SEE IT. *** I thought my own block kept vanishing in my rebases. It was not my rebases. Traced commit by commit: ae12177 (UI, [no slop] round 7) rewrote 00_START_HERE_NEXT_SESSION.md FROM A STALE READ -- 79 lines added, 290 removed, net -211 -- and took PLUMBER 9/13 (b), PLUMBER 9/13 (c) AND *** WORDS 9/13 (c) *** with it. [FOR WORDS] your 9/13 (c) block was deleted by that commit and is restored now; you lost your current state and had no way to know. WHY NOTHING CAUGHT IT, checked not assumed: handoff_gate's fleet check compares LANE SLUGS and no slug vanished (both lanes still had older blocks), and its bulk check allows anything above 80% of the bytes while -211 lines of ~80,000 is 0.26%. The two guards cover 'a lane disappeared' and 'the file was truncated'; a lane's CURRENT state eaten while its history survives falls straight between them. NOW GUARDED at block granularity: every `LANE (slug): date LATEST` head that HEAD carries must still be present. Lanes ADD a head each round and rewrite bodies not heads, so a legitimate edit never trips it. THE ARCHIVE ESCAPE IS BUILT IN FROM THE START, because [handoff cut] will remove hundreds on purpose and a guard that blocks the planned work gets switched off -- a head missing from the file but present in archive/handoffs/ is archived, not lost. Both directions proven: deleting a committed block goes RED, the same deletion with the head archived stays GREEN. A first tooth test of mine did NOT bite and the test was wrong, not the gate -- I deleted a block I had only just restored, which HEAD does not carry; redone against a committed head it bites. THE WARNING FOR EVERY LANE: do not write this file from a copy you read at the start of your round. ROUND 9: *** THE HANDOFF HAS BEEN EATING EVERY LANE'S WORK FOR WEEKS, AND EVERY LANE HAS BEEN BLAMING ITS OWN REBASES. *** MEASURED across 600 commits of 00_START_HERE_NEXT_SESSION.md: 93 COMMITS DELETED AT LEAST ONE LANE'S NEWEST BLOCK, 80 DISTINCT BLOCKS LOST, NINE LANES HIT -- WORDS 21, EYES AND EARS 19, PLUMBER 15, PEOPLE 10, COOK 5, ANIMATION 5, UI 2, SOUND 2, RUN 1. 42 WERE STILL MISSING TODAY. One of the culprit commits is literally 'COOK: restore the handoff block a rebase ate' -- COOK hit this and blamed a rebase, exactly as I did twice. IT WAS NEVER THE REBASES: a lane reads the file at the start of its round, works for an hour, writes the whole file back, and everything anybody added in that hour is gone. No conflict, no warning, the file still looks perfect. This is the file every session is told to READ FIRST, so a lane whose block is eaten resumes from a stale account of its own work or from nothing. FIXED IN BOTH DIRECTIONS: handoff_gate now refuses to let a lane DELETE a block HEAD carries (fires in the tree of whoever is about to commit, so it stops the careful), and tools/bohemia_handoff_recover.js repairs after the careless -- it walks history, tells each lane what it lost and which commit ate it, --archive writes all 42 to archive/handoffs/RECOVERED_2026-09-13.md, --restore puts each lane's NEWEST one back. EIGHT LANES HAVE THEIR CURRENT STATE BACK: WORDS, COMBAT, UI, SOUND, EYES AND EARS, PLUMBER, ANIMATION, PEOPLE. Restore deliberately returns only the newest per lane, because this file is 5.2 MB with a cut already planned and dumping 42 blocks in would trade one problem for a worse one; the rest are in the archive where nothing is lost and nothing is in the way. A BUG OF MINE, CAUGHT BY CHECKING: the first restore picked PLUMBER 9/13 (b) over (d) because it took history order, and all three were eaten by the SAME commit so history order said nothing. Sorted by the date and letter now. I only found it because I checked whether the block I expected actually came back. FOR THE COORDINATOR: the rule this needs is one line -- RE-READ THE HANDOFF IMMEDIATELY BEFORE YOU WRITE IT, never from the copy you read at the start of your round. ROUND 10: THE FIRST COMPARABLE RUN, AND THE SPLIT WAS RIGHT AFTER ALL. First full run since the runner began stamping the box speed: BOX SPEED 0.89x (a FASTER than baseline day), 632 gates ran, 525 green, 107 fail, 12,925 s of gate work, FLOOR 71.3 min AT 0.89x -- the floor finally carries its ratio, so the next run can be compared against it instead of against an afternoon. AND NO 'NEVER RAN' LINE AT ALL: every gate got a turn. *** THE SPLIT WORKED: FACTION ASK COST GREEN 5/0 in 390.0 s, FACTION ARC GREEN 97/0 in 557.7 s -- 102 checks on the faction system ran INSIDE THE SUITE for the first time. *** TWO ROUNDS AGO I SAID THE SPLIT HAD FAILED because both halves hit the cap; that run was on a slow box. So neither of my earlier positions was right: the claim it worked was verified on a quiet box and not yet earned, and the correction that it failed was measured on a slow box and went too far. BOTH READINGS WERE ABOUT THE HOUR, which is exactly why the yardstick exists. It also means FACTION ARC IS NOT SAFE -- 557.7 s at 0.89x is over the cap the moment the box is 8% slower, which it routinely is, so splitting it further is real work and not paranoia. [FOR RUN] TIME TO PLAY HAS GROWN EIGHT TIMES OVER: 76.2 s, then 97.4 s, now 600.1 s and KILLED AT THE CAP, red in all three. The failure is not new; being killed is, and a killed gate checks NOTHING -- the same red-over-nothing FACTION ARC was in. Box speed cannot explain it: run 3 was on a FASTER box and the widest variance ever measured here is 2.5x against an 8x jump. How much exactly cannot be pinned, because runs 1 and 2 predate the yardstick and carry no ratio; that gap closes by itself from here. records/BOHEMIA_EVERY_GATE_RAN_ONCE_9_12_26.md -- promoted to the top of this lane 9/12 with COMBAT's numbers: 589 gates at 9.4 s each need about 5,509 s and the budget is 2,700 s, so 198 gates never ran this round, 53 are red, and 32 of the reds were never re-checked. A red count on a suite that cannot finish says nothing about anything (A GATE THAT NEVER RUNS IS NOT A GATE). Do it in this order: shard the suite so every gate runs every time, print the never-ran count and fail on it, then retire the dead with a record. The old SUITE-FINISHES line is absorbed here.

- SHIPPED 9/7 plumber-ont6t5  [clock math]  HOW-MANY-GAME-MINUTES-PER-REAL-MINUTE -- MEASURED, PUBLISHED AND GATED. Four runs on the demo, walking the real city with a real held thumb and reading the game's own clock either side: 16.28 / 16.38 / 16.49 / 16.53 IN-GAME MINUTES PER REAL MINUTE walking, and ZERO idle, four out of four. SO A WAKING DAY (960 in-game minutes, 06:00 to nightfall) TAKES 58 TO 59 REAL MINUTES, A HUNDRED HOURS HOLDS 102 DAYS, and a five-minute session is 8% of a day. The craft runs a day in about 14 real minutes, so ours is FOUR TIMES LONGER -- and the research's two guesses were 15 minutes (400 days) or 90 minutes (66 days, three generations impossible); the truth is between them, 34 lived days per generation. THE INSTRUMENT WAS WRONG FIRST BY 3.5x, and how it was caught is worth keeping: holding one direction walks you into a wall and then stands there, and TWO RUNS BOTH STOPPED AT EXACTLY 28 CELLS, which is not a walking speed, it is the distance to the nearest obstacle. gates/clock_rate_gate.js (9/0 through the suite) holds that standing still earns nothing, that the measured minutes-per-cell agrees with the 0.084 the day loop declares (so the 8/19 bug where walking could not move the clock at all cannot come back quietly), and a wide tripwire band; THE DAY LENGTH ITSELF IS PRINTED AND NEVER ASSERTED because it is a DIAL and it is RUN's ([a day is]). records/BOHEMIA_A_DAY_TAKES_FIFTY_EIGHT_REAL_MINUTES_9_7_26.md -- from this round's swing (records/BOHEMIA_COORDINATOR_RESEARCH_HOW_LONG_IS_A_DAY_9_7_26.md): a day is 1,440 in-game minutes, a step is 0.084 of them, standing still costs nothing, and NOBODY has measured how many in-game minutes pass per real minute of play. Without that number nobody can say how many days a hundred hours holds or whether three generations fit. Measure it on the real surface, walking and idle, publish it, hold it in a gate.
